## GEN_REFUGEES_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AGEN_REFUGEES_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在扫描[Root.GetName]时截获了一个信号。对其模式分析后，初步判断是一艘舰船发出的求救信号，需要追踪其来源以获得更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

As we scanned [Root.GetName] we received a signal. Pattern analysis makes it out to be a distress call from a ship, but we cannot be sure. We need to track the signal to its source if we wish to know more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:37`

## DES_PLANET_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADES_PLANET_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的高温尚在预料范围之内。但是，我们的队伍还收集到了很多反常数据。在完成最终报告之前，还需要深入研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

The scorching heat of [Root.GetName] is within the expected range. However, there is more unusual data still being collected by our team. Further study is needed before a final report can be filed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:43`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上面的一些植物在自然选择中进化出了一套有效的自我防御措施。许多的树木和真菌都会释放出孢子，这些孢子对非植物生命来说具有非常强的酸性。还好，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]发现了中和其中一些孢子的方法，让这颗星球上的森林不那么致命了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

It appears that some of [From.GetName]'s flora has developed effective defense mechanisms through natural selection. Numerous trees and fungi exude spores that act like a powerful acidic on non-plant organisms. Thankfully Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has found a way to counteract some strands of these spores, making the planet's forests less threatening to us!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:57`

## anomaly.4000.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4000.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

£blocker£ 剧毒沼泽§R+2§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

£blocker£ Noxious Swamp §R+2§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:58`

## anomaly.4005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

生命残片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

Fragments of Life

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:59`

## anomaly.4005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看来，小行星[From.GetName]及其周围碎片来源于一个已经消亡的星球！\n\n在小行星中心，我们可以找到极具价值的合金的痕迹。同时，这里还找到了一些已死于在真空中的细菌曾经的活动迹象。这一切表明了这个天体曾孕育过生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

It appears that the asteroid [From.GetName] and the surrounding debris originate from a former planet!\n\nDeep within the center of the asteroid we can find traces of extremely valuable alloys. There are also hints of bacterial life that has now died in the vacuum of space. This shows us that the celestial body once bore the promise of life.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:60`

## anomaly.4005.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4005.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

把§Y曾经的行星§!添加到[From.GetName]上：\n£alloys£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

Adds the §YEx-Planet§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£alloys£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:61`

## anomaly.4010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷失太空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

Lost in Space

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:62`

## anomaly.4010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们接收到的信号来自于一艘围绕[From.GetName]旋转的舰船。这艘船现在没有任何热成像，似乎是在漂浮。我们已经制定了一个§S特殊项目§!来调查船员的遭遇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

The signal we intercepted was a distress call from a ship in orbit around [From.GetName]. The ship is not giving off any heat signatures and seems to be drifting. A §SSpecial Project§! has been issued to investigate what has happened to the crew.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:63`

## anomaly.4011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

舰上的船员已经全部丧生。更令人不安的是，他们似乎是被某种高度发达的脑寄生虫杀死的。它们寄生在船员的脑子里并使其疯狂。多亏了科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]及时发现，我们的船员才没有被感染。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:62`

### English

The crew of the ship has been found dead. What is more disturbing is that they appear to have been killed by some highly advanced brain parasites, which infested them and drove them mad. Thanks to Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s findings we were able to protect our own crew from being infected!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:64`

## anomaly.4011.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4011.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

把这些尸体送回[Owner.Capital.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:63`

### English

Send the corpses back to [Owner.Capital.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:65`

## anomaly.4012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

围绕[From.From.From.GetName]运行的舰船并没有被遗弃。上面的船员还活着，但是状态不好。他们受到了某种脑寄生虫的影响。我们的科学家能够轻松地治愈他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

The ship in orbit around [From.From.From.GetName] was not abandoned. The crew is alive, but not well, influenced by some sort of brain parasite. With the help of our scientists they could easily be cured.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:66`

## anomaly.4012.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4012.a&lang=en)

### 中文

帮助他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:65`

### English

Help them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:67`

## anomaly.4012.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4012.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

与外星人建立联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:66`

### English

Establish contact with the aliens.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:68`

## anomaly.4012.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4012.b&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们自生自灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:67`

### English

Let them perish.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:69`

## anomaly.4015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后休憩

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:68`

### English

The Final Rest

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:70`

## anomaly.4015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管宛如大海捞针，[Root.GetName]精确地找出了那个穿过了小行星带的奇怪信号的来源。信号来源于一个小型外星物品。简单的扫描显示出它是一种仪式性的容器，里面装着某位外星宇航员的遗体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:69`

### English

By what has to be beyond astronomical odds, the [Root.GetName] actually managed to pinpoint the source of the odd signals as it soared past through the asteroid belt. A tiny, alien construct. A simple scan reveals that it is some sort of a ritualistic container, holding the remains of an alien spacefarer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:71`

## anomaly.4015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让那个棺材继续它的旅途吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:70`

### English

Let the coffin continue its voyage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:72`

## anomaly.4015.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.b&lang=en)

### 中文

打开棺材，研究那具尸体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:71`

### English

Open the coffin and study the corpse.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:73`

## anomaly.4015.b.evopred

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.b.evopred&lang=en)

### 中文

有很多值得学习的东西。打开荚舱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:72`

### English

There is much to learn. Open the pod.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:74`

## anomaly.4015.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4015.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

把它带回[Root.Owner.Capital.GetName]，放在博物馆里面展出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:73`

### English

Bring it to [Root.Owner.Capital.GetName] and display it in a museum.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:75`

## anomaly.4016.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[DefiledCountry.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

[DefiledCountry.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:76`

## anomaly.4016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

距离我们和[DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]第一次接触已经过去几天了，现在他们向我们发来了一则消息：\n\n“我们知道你们做了什么，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。你们开启的那个棺材是我们第一位星际宇航员最后的休憩之处。我们要求你们道歉！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:75`

### English

A couple of days have passed since we first made contact with the [DefiledCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural], and now they are sending us a message:\n\n"We know what you have done, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. The casket you desecrated was the final resting place of our first star traveler. We demand an apology!"

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:77`

## anomaly.4016.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

你们之间的关系会受到影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:76`

### English

Your relationship will be affected.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:78`

## anomaly.4016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为冒犯事件道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:77`

### English

We apologize for this transgression.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:79`

## anomaly.4016.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.b&lang=en)

### 中文

那只是个尸体罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

It is merely a dead body.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:80`

## anomaly.4016.c.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.c.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会为你们祖先的灵魂祈祷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:79`

### English

We will pray for the spirit of your ancestor.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:81`

## anomaly.4016.d.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4016.d.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

异星杂种，我们根本就不在乎！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:80`

### English

We could not care less, alien scum!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:82`

## anomaly.4020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

巨舰陨落之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

Leviathan Down

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:83`

## anomaly.4020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过彻底的扫描，我们发现了[From.GetName]奇异矿石成分的成因。[From.GetPlanetMoon]是几十艘巨型太空船的坠毁地点，其船体之大远远超出我们的想象。它们建造的时间可以追溯到几百万年前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:82`

### English

Thorough scans have revealed the cause of [From.GetName]'s odd metal composition. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] is the crash site of dozens of massive spaceships, larger than anything we could have ever imagined. Their construction dates back several million years.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:84`

## anomaly.4020.rocket.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.rocket.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]发现了那些奇怪的矿物读数的源头。[From.GetName]是巨型宇宙舰船的坟场，这里的舰船残骸已经沉睡了几百万年。[Root.GetLeaderName]还说道，“真是太遗憾了，这些推进器损坏过于严重，仔细检测也不会有什么结果。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:83`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has found the source of the odd mineral readings. [From.GetName] is a graveyard for gargantuan spaceships that have been resting there for millions of years. [Root.GetLeaderName] goes on and on in the debriefing about a "profound sadness that the rockets are too broken down for even careful examination to yield results."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:85`

## anomaly.4020.rocket.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.rocket.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现那些奇怪的矿物读数来源于一些数量不明，异常巨大且技术先进的太空船残骸。飞船部件的锈蚀情况表明这片残骸于大约2,380,669年前产生。现场的科研单位试图对这些舰船的推进器进行逆向工程，但因其破损过于严重而失败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:84`

### English

We have discovered that the source of the odd mineral readings are the remains of an unconfirmed number of abnormally large, technologically advanced spaceships. Material decomposition indicates an age of approximately 2 380 669 years. The science unit on site attempted to reverse engineer the vessels' rockets but failed due to their heavily damaged state.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:86`

## anomaly.4020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些材料会派上用场的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:85`

### English

The materials will surely come in handy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:87`

## anomaly.4020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.b&lang=en)

### 中文

至少这些材料还很有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:86`

### English

At least the materials will be useful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:88`

## anomaly.4020.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4020.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

把§Y$ship_graveyard$§!添加到[From.GetName]上：\n£alloys£ §G+2§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:87`

### English

Adds the §Y$ship_graveyard$§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£alloys£ §G+2§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:89`

## anomaly.4025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

老探险家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:88`

### English

Old Explorer

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:90`

## anomaly.4025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的科学队伍在[From.GetName]上的一个偏远的地方找到了一个与之格格不入的金属板。\n\n上面有关于一个遥远恒星系的一些基本信息，还有图示和一张类似于元素周期表的东西。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]成功地使用这些信息推断出恒星系的位置，以及其中潜在的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:89`

### English

Located in a remote part of [From.GetName], the science team of the [Root.GetName] has found an out-of-place metallic tablet.\n\nIt contains basic information about a distant system, using illustrations and what appears to be a periodic table. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has been able to use the information to deduce the location and potential resources of the system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:91`

## anomaly.4025.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4025.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

得到遥远恒星系的§Y调查数据§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:90`

### English

Gain full §YSurvey Data§! on the distant system.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:92`

## anomaly.4030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

沙之仆人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:91`

### English

The Servants of Sand

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:93`

## anomaly.4030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的沙丘下面，我们的科学团队找到了坠毁的战斗机舰队。虽然很古老，但是却保存的十分完好。它们早已经被遗弃，但是引擎和照明部件似乎还能运作。它们坠毁的原因目前尚不清楚。我们在旗舰的舰桥上面找到了舰队的人工智能，它能够在战斗中指挥这些船只，并且其仍在运作。我们靠近的时候，它拉响了警报。在入侵者靠近的时候它就会自动启动。\n我们能够施行一个§S特殊项目§!在这个人工智能发疯之前回收它。我们也可以毁了它，避免未知的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:92`

### English

Under the dunes of [From.GetName] the science team has found a fleet of crashed fighters. Ancient, yet well preserved. They are abandoned, but the lights and engines seem to function. Why they crashed is a mystery. On the main ship bridge we found a fleet AI, capable of controlling the ships in battle. It is still active. As we approach a warning signal calls. It is programmed to activate when threatened by intruders!\nA §SSpecial Project§! can be issued to try and salvage the AI before it goes haywire, or we could destroy it and avoid the risk.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:94`

## anomaly.4030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试着去控制这个人工智能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

Try and take control of the AI.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:95`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4030.b&lang=en)

在它试图做危险的事情之前毁了它。

### English

Destroy it before it tries anything.

## anomaly.4030.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4030.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

毁掉这个人工智能。\n\n把§Y坠毁的人工智能舰队§!修正添加到[From.GetName]上：\n£engineering£§G+2§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

Destroys the AI.\n\nAdds the §YCrashed AI-Fleet§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+2§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:97`

## anomaly.4031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地，我们成功地在人工智能攻击我们之前为它重新编写了程序。它目前已可作为我们舰队中的旗舰工作，并且如果我们愿意的话，它也可以成为指挥官。这个人工智能已经被运送到我们的母星，等待指派。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:96`

### English

Thankfully we were able to reprogram the AI before it turned on us. It has been repurposed to commandeer ships in our fleet, and could act as a sort of Commander if we so choose. The AI has been transported to our homeworld, awaiting assignment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:98`

## anomaly.4035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

炉中之火

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:97`

### English

Fire of the Furnace

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:99`

## anomaly.4035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetName]发现了有趣的东西，不过跟[From.GetPlanetMoon]本身没有什么关系。我们像往常一样进行测试时，突然捕获到一颗遥远的恒星系中的恒星传出的奇怪数据。该恒星的辐射表明，它可以在不发生散裂的情况下让铍原子发生聚变，而这根本就是不可能的！\n\n科学团队已经迫不及待想前去一探究竟了，而且他们已经将范围缩小到两个不是很远的恒星系。\n查询§S情报日志§!获取细节。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:98`

### English

We have made an interesting find on [From.GetName], however it is not related to the [From.GetPlanetMoon] itself. While conducting our usual tests we captured some odd data from a far-off system's star. Its radiation indicates that it is able to fuse together beryllium without spallation; something that should not be possible!\n\nThe science team are eager to investigate and have limited the potential stars down to two not-too-distant systems.\nConsult the §SSituation Log§! for details.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:100`

## anomaly.4035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这可能会带来丰厚的收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:99`

### English

This could prove fruitful.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:101`

## anomaly.4035.poi_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.poi_1&lang=en)

### 中文

第一个有可能的恒星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:100`

### English

First Potential Star

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:102`

## anomaly.4035.poi_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.poi_2&lang=en)

第二个有可能的恒星

### English

Second Potential Star

## anomaly.4035.poi.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4035.poi.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些恒星系中的一个可能有那颗发出奇怪辐射的恒星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

One of the systems that could potentially hold the odd radiation star.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:104`

## anomaly.4037.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4037.name&lang=en)

### 中文

正确的恒星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

Correct System

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:105`

## anomaly.4037.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4037.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们找到了正确的恒星系！这颗恒星的辐射数据和之前遇到的完全一致。我们制定了一个§S特殊项目§!来对这颗恒星进行研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:104`

### English

We seem to have found the right system! The radiation data of the local star match the ones previously encountered. A §SSpecial Project§! has been issued to perform research on the star.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:106`

## anomaly.4038.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4038.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了！科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]发现这个恒星是围绕一个小型虫洞形成的。虫洞撕开了一道维度裂缝，从其他维度来的射线代替了宇宙射线，使得元素的散裂无法发生。超新星辐射导致这颗恒星中形成的铍-8衰变成了更稳定的元素，因此这颗恒星内发生的核聚变十分特别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Eureka! Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has found that the star is formed around a miniscule wormhole, which has ripped open a dimensional rift. Rays from this other dimension has replaced the need of cosmic rays in spallation, the process of supernovae radiation reducing the beryllium-8 created in stars into a stable element. The star is thus unique in its fusion capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:107`

## anomaly.4038.maxed.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4038.maxed.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4038.base.desc$\n\n这些知识并不会使我们现有的科技得到提升。但是，它告诉了我们更多关于宇宙运行原理的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

$anomaly.4038.base.desc$\n\nThis knowledge won't expand our available technology. However, it grants us more insight into what makes the universe tick.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:108`

## anomaly.4038.upg.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4038.upg.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4038.base.desc$\n\n多亏了这个发现，我们的科学家现在能够研究新的科技了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

$anomaly.4038.base.desc$\n\nThanks to this discovery our scientists will be able to research new technology!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:109`

## anomaly.4038.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4038.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经掌握了恒星的奥秘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

We have mastered the secrets of suns.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:110`

## anomaly.4038.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4038.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正在逐步揭开宇宙神秘的面纱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

The mysteries of the universe are slowly revealed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:111`

## anomaly.4040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恒星之罪

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Sins of a Sun

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:112`

## anomaly.4040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的离子辐射强度已经爆表了。看起来[From.GetPlanetMoon]似乎最近受到了核弹头的轰炸，或者是暴露在了可能是从附近超新星所发出的聚集射线束之中。至于后者是否能够造成这种效果，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]毫无头绪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

The ionizing radiation on [From.GetName] is off the charts. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] seems to either have been bombarded with nuclear warheads quite recently or been exposed to a concentrated beam of cosmic rays, perhaps from a nearby supernova. How the latter could ever be achieved is beyond Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:113`

## anomaly.4040.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4040.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

把§R辐射废土§!修正添加到[From.GetName]上：\n£blocker£ 辐射废土 §R+6§!\n$mod_planet_jobs_physics_research_produces_mult$：§G+10%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Adds the §RIrradiated Wasteland§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£blocker£ Radioactive Wasteland §R+6§!\n$mod_planet_jobs_physics_research_produces_mult$: §G+10%§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:114`

## anomaly.4045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深处的回声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:113`

### English

Echoes from the Deep

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:115`

## anomaly.4045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过缜密研究之后，[Root.GetName]的成员探测到了一个“微弱”的回声正在从[From.GetName]的地底深处传来。\n\n这种回声有点像是生命体发出的。可是我们与它的距离实在是太远了，很难分辨它是真的，还是只是一台发出随机信号的设备。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]的手下一直在抱怨说自己得不到适当的休息，工作时间又太长，自己再也受不了了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:114`

### English

After rigorous research, the crew of [Root.GetName] has only been able to detect a faint "echo" emanating deep beneath the surface of [From.GetName].\n\nThe echo resembles a life signature, however it is so distant it is hard to tell if it is real or just the instruments giving off random variations. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s crew is complaining about bad rest and long hours and cannot take much more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:116`

## anomaly.4045.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.a&lang=en)

### 中文

校正探测仪，再好好看看星球的内核！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:115`

### English

Calibrate the scanners and look closer to the core!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:117`

## anomaly.4045.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.b&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们不能相信这些船员。我们需要一次实地考察！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

"The crew cannot be trusted. An expedition is needed!"

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:118`

## anomaly.4045.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.c&lang=en)

### 中文

船员的健康是第一位的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

The crew's health comes first.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:119`

## anomaly.4045.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4045.d&lang=en)

### 中文

在弄清楚之前，我们绝不能停下！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

We cannot rest until we get to the bottom of this!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:120`

## anomaly.4046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管大家都筋疲力尽了，但是在调整了舰船的探测仪后，[Root.GetName]的船员和科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]得到了一个惊人的发现。在[From.From.GetName]的内核附近，有一种类似于浮游生物的生命体。它们分布于整个[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]，有着共同的意识，以及强大的灵能能力。\n\n这种生命体能够在极高的压力和温度下存活，甚至可以以极高的速率进行繁衍。它看起来像是在冬眠……或者是在躲避什么东西，就好像它知道自己正在被观测一样。\n\n对于如此巨大而拥有诸多优势的生命体，理论上来说它是永生的。不过，它看起来并不会妨碍我们对[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]进行殖民。但是它可能会对上面的居民产生一些负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

After doing some adjustments to the ship scanners, the crew of [Root.GetName] and Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], although exhausted and on edge, have made a startling discovery. Close to the core of [From.From.GetName] is a plankton-like lifeform that stretches across the entire [From.From.GetPlanetMoon], living in a shared consciousness with vast psionic abilities.\n\nThe lifeform is able to survive under both extreme pressures and temperatures. It can even expand itself at rapid rates. It seem to either be hibernating or... hiding, as if it knew it was being watched.\n\nA lifeform this vast in size and versatile seems technically immortal. However it does not seem like it would hinder us to colonize the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon], but it might have a negative influence on the population.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:121`

## anomaly.4046.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4046.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该试着去和它接触。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

We should try and contact it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:122`

## anomaly.4046.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4046.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以向海中下毒并杀死它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

We could poison the ocean to kill it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:123`

## anomaly.4046.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4046.c&lang=en)

### 中文

最好还是不要理它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Better to leave it alone.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:124`

## anomaly.4046.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4046.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§R负面媒体报道§!修正。\n\n把§R恶劣环境§!修正添加到[From.From.GetName]上。\n\n把§Y远古造物§!修正添加到[From.From.GetName]上：\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §RNegative Media Coverage§! modifier.\n\nAdds the §RHostile Environment§! modifier to [From.From.GetName].\n\nAdds the §YAncient One§! modifier to [From.From.GetName]:\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:125`

## anomaly.4047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在多次尝试接触而没有结果后，我们的小队决定尝试用声波进行联系。声波使那个生物产生了巨大的反应，所有的小队成员都双膝跪地，陷入了栩栩如生的幻象之中。\n\n这好像是这种生物的主要交流方式。它描述了它的母星，它静静地躺在星核附近，沐浴在[From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon]的温暖之中，在黑暗中繁衍。然后它向我们展示了我们的人民站在地表上，在阳光中繁衍壮大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:124`

### English

After trying several methods of contact without an answer the team decides to try sonar pings. The sound waves cause a massive reaction in the being and every crew member falls to their knees, struck with vivid visions.\n\nThis seems to be the creatures' main form of communication. It depicts its homeworld, where it lies alone near the core basking in the warmth of the [From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon], thriving in the darkness. Then it shows our people, standing on the surface, thriving in the sunshine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:126`

## anomaly.4047.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4047.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很乐意接受这份邀请。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

We gladly accept this invitation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:127`

## anomaly.4047.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4047.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§R负面媒体报道§!修正。\n\n把§Y远古造物§!修正添加到[From.From.From.GetName]上：\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §RNegative Media Coverage§! modifier.\n\nAdds the §YAncient One§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName]:\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:128`

## anomaly.4048.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4048.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]很快就合成出了一种毒药，它应该会和这个生物的内在结构产生剧烈的反应，以野火之势扩散开来。\n\n在释放了毒药之后，船员们陷入到了极大的恐惧之中。他们目睹了一个史无前例的事件。[From.From.From.GetName]的内核温度急剧升高，导致整个海洋沸腾。生命体那巨大的躯体也开始向大气中释放毒素。这个浮游生物般的东西对于[From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon]那不稳定的核心来说一定是至关重要的。这种毒素应该也是它在进化中得到的一种自我防御的机制。\n\n[From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon]在几个小时之内就充满了剧毒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:127`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] quickly synthesizes a poison that should react well with the creature's interconnected form and spread like wildfire.\n\nAfter releasing the poison the crew stare in horror as they bear witness to an unprecedented event. [From.From.From.GetName]'s core temperature spirals out of control which causes the oceans to boil. The creature's gargantuan body also releases toxins into the atmosphere. The plankton entity must have been vital in the regulation of the [From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon]'s unstable center, as well as developed toxic defense mechanisms in order to protect itself.\n\nThe [From.From.From.GetPlanetMoon] becomes completely toxic in a matter of hours.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:129`

## anomaly.4048.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4048.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§R负面媒体报道§!修正\n\n[From.From.From.GetName]的§Y行星类型§!变为§R剧毒§!。\n\n将§R恶劣环境§!修正添加到[From.From.From.GetName]上。\n\n在[From.From.From.GetName]上添加资源：\n£society£ §G+4§!

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §RNegative Media Coverage§! modifier.\n\nThe §YPlanet Class§! of [From.From.From.GetName] changes to §RToxic§!.\n\nAdds the §RHostile Environment§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName].\n\nAdds resources to [From.From.From.GetName]:\n£society£ §G+4§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:131`

## anomaly.4049.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4049.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于怀疑这些船员想要隐瞒什么，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]决定发起一场不经批准的单枪匹马的探险，以仔细检查星球的内核。船上潜艇的摄像机传回了录像带，记录了事情的经过：\n\n深入[From.From.GetName]的海洋，那里只有黑暗。但是，还有一些……活着的东西。在[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]温暖的内核附近有一些深海洞穴。洞穴的里面有网状的、类似于浮游生物的植被，缠绕着[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]的整个地壳。\n\n在调查浮游生物的时候，[Root.GetLeaderName]突然癫痫发作，在几个小时之后才醒过来。\n\n回到[Root.GetName]后，科学专员神志不清，一直在诉说一些关于恐惧和邪恶幻境的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

Suspicious that the crew is hiding something, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] decided to mount an unauthorized solo-expedition to examine the planet's core. A video feed from the ships submarine shows what happened:\n\nGoing deep into the oceans of [From.From.GetName] there is only darkness, but also something... alive. Inside deep-sea caverns close to the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon]'s warm core there are webs of plankton-like vegetation, that spans the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon]'s entire crust.\n\nWhile investigating the plankton [Root.GetLeaderName] is stricken with a seizure, only to awake hours later.\n\nAfter returning to the [Root.GetName] the Science Officer is delusional and only speaks of horrors and haunted visions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:132`

### English

## anomaly.4050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]的那次事件过去一个星期后，[Root.GetName]的船员被一声惊尖叫惊扰。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]大喊大叫，在船上横冲直撞，说自己感到很痛很痛。\n\n船员们把他带到医务室给他打了大量镇定剂并仔细检查后确认[Root.GetLeaderName]的大脑发生了改变。\n\n详细的检查最终发现这位领袖现在拥有了心灵感应能力，能够通过精神跟他人沟通。[Root.GetLeaderName]经过休息后会康复的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:135`

### English

It has been a week since the incident on [From.From.From.GetName] when the crew of the [Root.GetName] are disturbed by a loud scream. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is yelling, running through the halls of the ship, going on about inexplicable pains.\n\nThe crew brings the poor scientist to the sickbay and after heavy sedation and a thorough examination concludes that [Root.GetLeaderName]'s brain has changed.\n\nAfter further tests the team startlingly realizes that their leader is now capable of telekinesis and communicating with others via telepathy. [Root.GetLeaderName] is going to get healthy, after some rest.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:137`

### English

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]获得了§G灵能专精§!和§G异星相关专精§!特质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:138`

### English

## anomaly.4051.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4051.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员结束了他们的[From.From.GetName]研究，终于能够休息整顿了。可惜他们并没有什么重大收获，但是他们受到的良好待遇很快传开了，在母星深获好评。足够的休息确保了船员们能够在未来取得更好的结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s crew ends their research of [From.From.GetName], thankful of finally getting some rest. Sadly there were not any useful finds, but news of the crew being well-treated spreads fast and brings forth many a good word on our homeworld. Being well-rested should also have the crew produce even better results in the long run.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:141`

## anomaly.4051.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4051.a.tooltip&lang=en)

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§G正面媒体关注§!和§G休息充分的科学官§!修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §GPositive Media Coverage§! and §GWell Rested Scientists§! modifiers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:142`

## anomaly.4051.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4051.b.tooltip&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §GPositive Media Coverage§! and §GWell Rested Scientists§! modifiers.\n\nGives access to the §Y$edict_improved_work_environment$§! Empire Edict.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:143`

### English

The crew's well-being is its own reward.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4051.b&lang=en)

这是提高效率的办法之一。

### English

This is but one way to gain efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:145`

## anomaly.4052.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4052.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

夜以继日地研究回声的根源后，[Root.GetName]发现了[From.From.GetName]地面下生活的一种生物。之前没有发觉是因为它生活在非常靠近[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]核心的地方，这导致我们的系统分析对它毫无效用。\n\n通过扫描我们发现它是尚处于进化早期阶段的海洋节肢动物，但最神奇的是它竟然能够将死去的生物物质改造成自己的后代。\n\n这个信息非常有助于我们的科学家开发新的药物！不过，我们对待我们的人员的方式肯定会传播出去，造成不良影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:144`

### English

After working day and night in order to find the reason behind the echo, the crew of [Root.GetName] have found an interesting lifeform living underneath the surface of [From.From.GetName]. Previously we could not detect it due to its amazing ability to live so close to the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon]'s core, throwing off our systems analytics.\n\nScans show it to be a type of aquatic arthropod early in its evolution, but what is most surprising is the fact that it is able to reproduce by repurposing dead matter from other lifeforms into offspring.\n\nThis information will surely help our scientists to develop new pharmaceuticals! However, news of how we treated our personnel will surely spread and displease the general populace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:146`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §GPharmaceutical Innovation§! and §RNegative Media Coverage§! modifiers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:147`

## anomaly.4055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

璞玉浑金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

Diamond in the Rough

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:148`

## anomaly.4055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.GetName]的研究结束后，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报说他们发现这颗行星满是矿物。这个发现主要归功于[Root.GetName]的船员。[Root.GetLeaderName]对这位学徒赞赏有加。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

After the study of [From.GetName] has concluded, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that they have found the planet to be unusually rich in minerals. This discovery was made possible in large part thanks to a certain member of the [Root.GetName] crew. [Root.GetLeaderName] praises the protégé highly.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:149`

## anomaly.4060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

或然性难题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:153`

### English

The Probability Conundrum

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:155`

## anomaly.4060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]的报告。据说有一个……陶瓷容器在围绕着[From.GetName]的恒星旋转。真奇怪。\n\n这种东西不应该出现那儿，然而不管怎样它确实在近地轨道上。\n\n可以发布§S特殊项目§!来研究该容器并找出它出现在那里的原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

We have gotten a report from Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]. It seems that a... ceramic container is circling the star [From.GetName]. Most peculiar.\n\nIt should obviously not be there, yet somehow it has managed to find its way into a close orbit.\n\nA §SSpecial Project§! can be issued to investigate the container and try to deduce how it ended up there.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:156`

## anomaly.4060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得研究的难题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:155`

### English

A conundrum worth investigating.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:157`

## anomaly.4060.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4060.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不是所有问题都得有答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

All questions don't have worthwhile answers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:158`

## anomaly.4060.spirit.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4060.spirit.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这一定是神明显灵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Surely this is a sign of the divine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:159`

## anomaly.4061.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们竭尽所能试图找出这个陶瓷容器出现在恒星轨道的原因。也许这是我们的集体幻觉？也许它根本不存在？连这些可能性我们都计算过了。但是一无所获。我们尽力了。\n\n唯一的好处就是当我们再也无法忍受调查这东西时，我们可以慢慢研究哲学和经济问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

We have tried everything in order to find out how the ceramic pot ended up in a stellar orbit. Maybe we are all crazy? Maybe it does not even exist? Guess what? We tried calculating around those facts as well! We are done. There is nothing more we can do here.\n\nThe only plus side to this is that we had tons of time to spend on philosophical and economical questions, when we could not stomach to keep investigating that damned thing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:160`

## anomaly.4061.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

我们竭尽所能试图找出这个陶瓷容器出现在大空轨道的原因。或许我们都故障了？或许它根本就不存在？<<错误！>>连这些可能性我们都计算过了。<<错误！>>\n这事儿到此为止。我们尽力了。\n\n这项调查的一个好处就是，当我们无法容忍继续调查那个不合逻辑的罐子时，我们可以花大量的时间在理论与经济问题上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

We have tried everything in order to find out how the ceramic container ended up in a stellar orbit. Perhaps we are all malfunctioning? Perhaps it does not even exist? <<Error!>> We attempted to calculate around those facts as well. <<Error!>>\nWe are done. There is nothing more we can do here.\n\nThe one positive aspect of this investigation is that we had excessive amounts of time to spend on theoretical and economical questions, when we could not bear to keep investigating that illogical pot.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:161`

## anomaly.4061.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.a&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么？为什么！？为什么！？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Why? Why!? WHY!?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:162`

## anomaly.4061.a.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.a.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

我的电路好痛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

My circuits hurt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:163`

## anomaly.4061.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

接受解决生活问题的尝试常常徒劳无功的事实。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Embrace the futility of life's questions.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:164`

## anomaly.4061.a.tooltip.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4061.a.tooltip.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

接受存在的无意义性。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Embrace the futility of existence.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:165`

## anomaly.4062.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4062.base&lang=en)

### 中文

在几乎要放弃继续研究这可恶的陶瓷物体时，我们终于取得了突破！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

After almost giving up hope, studying the accursed ceramic object for so long, we have reached a breakthrough!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:166`

## anomaly.4062.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4062.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

在花了大量时间研究那个陶瓷物体却并未取得明显进展之后，我们的科学单元终于有了突破。该物体并不存在于我们[Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]所知的三维空间中，而是一个来自更高维度的投影。\n\n分析这个四维物体的本影让我们积累了足够的数据来解密它的信息。该物体教会我们如何在所有科研领域取得进展。\n\n现在只剩下一个问题：是谁，或者是什么东西发送了这个信息？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:165`

### English

After an extensive period of studying the confounding ceramic object without notable results, our scientific units have finally reached a breakthrough. The object does not exist in what we [Root.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] know as three-dimensional spacetime, but rather it is a projection from a higher dimension.\n\nAnalyzing it, as an umbra of a fourth dimensional object, we have accumulated enough data to decrypt its message. The object teaches us how to attain improvements in all fields of research.\n\nOnly one question remains: Who or what sent this information?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:167`

## anomaly.4062.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4062.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4062.base$该物体并非真是存在于那里，而是从更高级的维度投影下来的。分析发现，那是一个四维物体的影子，我们累积了足够的数据去解码它的信息。该物体能够改善我们对所有研究领域的认知。\n\n现在唯一的问题是……究竟是谁发来的这道信息？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

$anomaly.4062.base$ The object isn't really there, but rather it is a projection from a higher dimension. Analyzing it, as a shadow of a fourth dimensional object, we have accumulated enough data to decrypt its message. The object teaches us how to improve in all fields of research.\n\nThe only question now is... who sent this information?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:168`

## anomaly.4062.spirit.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4062.spirit.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4062.base$该容器是一个神圣遗物，无疑是由神灵留在轨道上的信号！\n\n该物体散发着能量，解码后终于显示出隐藏的信息：那是能够改善我们的研究能力以助我们尽早升天的信息！\n\n现在唯一的问题是……究竟是谁发来的这道信息？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

$anomaly.4062.base$ The container is a sacred relic, left in orbit as a sign, surely from the divine!\n\nThe object is brimming with energy, which when decrypted reveals its hidden message: information on how to help us ascend by improving our research efforts!\n\nThe only question now is... who sent this message?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:169`

## anomaly.4062.spirit.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4062.spirit.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:168`

### English

Thanks to the powers that be!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:170`

## anomaly.4065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发掘矿机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:169`

### English

Mining Your Own Business

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:171`

## anomaly.4065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在巨型小行星[From.GetName]的地表下，我们发现了让人叹为观止的东西。有一台采矿设备安置在岩体上，一直挖到小行星地核深处！看情况它是被遗弃了，没有任何痕迹显示是谁留下的。\n\n如果我们能够把这部机器停下，我们也许能够从中学习到制造它的科技，但一定要趁早，免得它被岩屑什么的压碎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

Deep within a crevice of the giant asteroid [From.GetName] we have found something astonishing. There is mining gear attached to the rock, digging itself into the vast depth of the asteroid! It stands abandoned, with no sign of who placed it there.\n\nIf we can stop the machine we might be able to learn something about the technology used to build it, but we must hurry before random debris crushes it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:172`

## anomaly.4065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

试着把机器停下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Try and stop the machine.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:173`

## anomaly.4066.upg.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4066.upg.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功在挖掘机损坏之前制止了它。真是个神奇的机器，它的平滑的表层完全被钻石所覆盖。乍看之下，它似乎是一个整体，但其实只是做工精良，让人难以发现其连接处。\n\n我们的工程师可以对它的一些科技进行逆向工程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

We managed to stop the excavator before it broke down. It's a remarkable piece of machinery, with a smooth surface covered in diamonds. At first glance the whole drill seems like one solid piece, but it's simply so well put together that you cannot notice the grooves and joints.\n\nOur engineers will be able to reverse engineer some of this technology.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:174`

## anomaly.4066.res.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4066.res.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功在挖掘机损坏之前制止了它。可惜它其实跟我们当前的设计非常类似，没有什么值得学习的。不过它挖出的洞可以让我们更轻易地找到贵重矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

We managed to stop the excavator before it broke down. Sadly it is close to our own designs, and we can't really learn anything new from it. Thankfully the depths it carved out into the rock will make it easier to find valuable minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:175`

## anomaly.4066.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4066.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.From.From.GetName]添加资源：\n£minerals£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

Adds resources to [From.From.From.GetName]:\n£minerals£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:176`

## anomaly.4070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

丰厚收获

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

Fruitful Endeavors

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:177`

## anomaly.4070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好消息！我们发现[From.GetName]的热带雨林中生长着一种特殊的树，它的果实非常有营养。这种食物含有着惊人的能量以及我们的人民所需的全部营养。这确定无疑地有助于减轻我们殖民这个地方的压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:176`

### English

Good news! We have found the tropical forests of [From.GetName] to be host to a special kind of tree, blooming with nutritious fruits. The food produced stores tons of energy and contains all the chemicals our people need to function well. This will most assuredly make life a little bit easier to sustain, would we want to colonize.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:178`

## anomaly.4070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4070.a&lang=en)

### 中文

的确是好消息！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

Good news indeed!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:179`

## anomaly.4075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

小体量，大力量

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

Strength from Small Places

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:180`

## anomaly.4075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

勘测过[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]的冰冻地面后，我们获得了重大发现。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]报告说，发现了一种独一无二的细菌生物。它会跟其他个体连接起来组成巨大的“毯子”，这种毯子既轻便耐用，又能用作太阳能电池。它们能够非常高效地转化吸收到的太阳能——似乎是为了在这种环境下尽可能吸收多的热能而进化出来的。\n\n我们的研究人员一定能够从这样的情报中发掘到可用之物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

Having probed the frozen landscapes of the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] we think we have struck something big. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] speaks of a bacterial lifeform unlike any other. It bonds with other individuals to form large "blankets" that are durable yet light, and also act like solar cells. They absorb solar energy with surprising efficiency - most likely a result of evolution favoring those who could gather more heat in the harsh climate.\n\nOur researchers back home could surely find some use for this information.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:181`

## anomaly.4075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

分析它是否能够改善能量生产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

Find out if it can improve energy production.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:182`

## anomaly.4075.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4075.b&lang=en)

### 中文

试图寻找军事用途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Try and find a military use.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:183`

## anomaly.4075.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4075.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

送回去母星作研究。需要一段时间才能出结果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Send to our homeworld for study. Results will take a while to process.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:184`

## anomaly.4076.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4076.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的能源开发研究员从我们发过去的[From.From.GetName]样本中取得了进展！他们利用该生物为模板，成功开发了一种新的太阳能电池。这种电池能够更高效地储存能量并提高我们所有发电站的效率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Our energy development researchers have made progress with the samples we sent them from [From.From.GetName]! They have been able to develop a new type of solar cell using the organisms as a template. These are much more efficient at storing energy and will provide a boost to all our power plants.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:185`

## anomaly.4077.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4077.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的军事开发研究员从我们发过去的[From.From.GetName]样本中取得了进展！他们改造了生物太阳能电池并分析了它们是如何储存热能而让自身不受伤害。这将有助于开发一种全新的舰队防御技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:184`

### English

Our military development researchers have made progress with the samples we sent them from [From.From.GetName]! They have reconstructed the biological solar cells and documented how they retain heat energy within themselves without taking damage. This has helped them develop a new defensive technology for our fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:186`

## anomaly.4080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遗弃的传感阵列

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

Abandoned Sensor Array

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:187`

## anomaly.4080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一座传感阵列，孤零零的俯瞰着[From.GetName]。这颗[From.GetPlanetMoon]上没有任何值得一提的智慧生物，所以这一定是座被遗弃的哨站。它的年代不算久远，但却无法使用了。\n\n我们需要修理并研究它才能让它恢复运作，不过要是拆毁它也许能够让我们学习到新的技术。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

We have found a sensor array, solemnly standing watch on [From.GetName]. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] has no sapient life to speak of, so it must be an abandoned outpost. It was built recently, but is not functioning.\n\nIt would require both repairs and research to bring it back into working condition, while breaking it apart could allow us to learn something new from it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:188`

## anomaly.4080.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4080.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试启动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:187`

### English

Try to activate it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:189`

## anomaly.4080.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4080.b&lang=en)

### 中文

拆毁并对其实施逆向工程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

Dismantle and reverse engineer it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:190`

## anomaly.4080.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4080.c&lang=en)

### 中文

不管它，也许它的主人会归来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Leave it alone in case the owner returns.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:191`

## anomaly.4081.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4081.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

数个月的工作终于开花结果了！[From.From.From.GetName]的传感阵列再一次恢复了运作，现在我们在附近一带的扫描范围更大了。不过传感阵列需要更多的能量和人力才能正常运行，所以我们需要建立一个殖民地来维持它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

Months of work have finally paid off! The sensor array on [From.From.From.GetName] is once again functioning and it appears to be able to give us a vastly improved sensor range of the nearby area. However, the array needs more energy and personnel to work, so a colony is needed in order to supply it with energy.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:192`

## anomaly.4081.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4081.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§G高级传感阵列§!修正到[From.From.From.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Adds the §GSuperior Sensor Array§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:193`

## anomaly.4081.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4081.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁§Y$edict_super_sensors$§!帝国法令。\n\n添加§G高级传感阵列§!修正到[From.From.From.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_super_sensors$§! Empire Edict.\n\nAdds the §GSuperior Sensor Array§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:194`

## anomaly.4085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

漂泊

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Adrift

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:195`

## anomaly.4085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在围绕着[From.GetName]的小行星中发现了一艘外星飞船在那里漂泊。它对我们的联络新号毫无反应。它到底是无法解析我们的信号，还是可能因为其他原因无视我们，我们无从得知。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]建议我们派遣一艘工程船来，将其拖出碎片区。要是不管它的话，这艘外星飞船很可能会被小行星撞碎。\n\n已发布一项§S特殊项目§!去拯救飞船并调查它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

We have discovered an alien ship adrift among the asteroids surrounding [From.GetName]. It is not responding to our hailing signals. Whether this is due to it being unable to interpret our signal, or perhaps some other reason, we do not know.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] recommends that a construction ship be dispatched to tow this stricken vessel out of the debris field. If left untouched, there is a risk the alien ship may be crushed by the asteroids.\n\nA §SSpecial Project§! has been issued to rescue and investigate the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:196`

## anomaly.4086.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4086.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

漂泊在[From.From.From.GetName]的飞船被拖拉出来，我们得以派遣特遣队去调查它。里面发生过一场谋杀。只找到了一位船员的尸体——那是一个长耳朵的两栖双腿生物，他身上有多处刺伤。该舰船因为没有了主人，已归我们所有。\n\n该舰船拥有非常高效的推进器，它的架构和设计都高于我们当前的水准。飞船没有装备武器，看来是一艘运送重要人员的运输船。\n\n把它改造成勘探船无需花费多大功夫。不过，如果我们拆毁它并仔细研究它的引擎，也许就能够对我们的舰队水平有实际提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Once the ship drifting by [From.From.From.GetName] was towed out of the debris, we were able to send in an away team to investigate. They uncovered a murder scene. Only one crew member has been found - a long-eared, amphibian biped who appears to have been stabbed repeatedly.\n\nWith its crew evidently dead, the ship is now up for grabs. It is fitted with very efficient thrusters, the structure and design of which are beyond us at present. The vessel is not armed, seemingly having been used as a transport for important personnel.\n\nRepurposing it into an exploration vessel would be a minor undertaking. However, if we deconstruct it and study the engines in greater detail, we may instead be able to apply its ingenious design to our own fleets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:197`

## anomaly.4087.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4087.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

漂泊在[From.From.From.GetName]的飞船被拖拉出来，我们得以派遣特遣队去调查它。上面的船员还活着！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

Once the ship drifting by [From.From.From.GetName] was towed out of the debris, we were able to send in an away team to investigate. The crew is still alive!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:198`

改造成科学考察船。

## anomaly.4090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

逃难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

On the Run

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:203`

## anomaly.4090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功追踪到信号了！[From.GetName]的轨道附近藏着另一艘船。船上的外星人回复了我们，但是我们都不明白彼此的语言。他们似乎是下面他们的这颗荒芜母星的难民，正在寻找新的家园求生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

We successfully traced the signal! Another ship drifts, hidden, in orbit around [From.GetName]. The aliens on board hailed us, yet we do not understand each other's languages. It appears that they are refugees from their barren homeworld below. They are clearly in desperate need of a new place to live.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:204`

## anomaly.4090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们居住在[refugee_planet_1.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:203`

### English

They can settle on [refugee_planet_1.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:205`

## anomaly.4090.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.b&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们居住在[refugee_planet_2.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Let them stay on [refugee_planet_2.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:206`

## anomaly.4090.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.c&lang=en)

让他们居住在[refugee_planet_3.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

They might fit in on [refugee_planet_3.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:207`

## anomaly.4090.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.d&lang=en)

让他们居住在[refugee_planet_4.GetName]。

### English

Let them move to [refugee_planet_4.GetName].

## anomaly.4090.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.e&lang=en)

### 中文

可惜，我们没有任何行星可以收容他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

Sadly we cannot fit them on any of our worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:209`

## anomaly.4090.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.f&lang=en)

抱歉，我们帮不了你们。

### English

Sorry, but we will not help you.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:210`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.g&lang=en)

我们不欢迎外星人！

### English

We will not play host to aliens!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:211`

## anomaly.4090.uninhabitable

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4090.uninhabitable&lang=en)

### 中文

很不幸，我们的星球无法适应他们的生存所需。

### English

Sadly, our worlds are inhospitable to their environmental needs.
