## anomaly.4093.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[RefugeeAliensNation.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:211`

### English

[RefugeeAliensNation.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:213`

## anomaly.4093.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经遇见过的缺乏互相沟通能力的外星难民，现在出现在我们的屏幕前。\n\n“我们两个种群再次相遇了，[From.GetSpeciesAdj]。现在我们终于找到了我们的新避风港，真是天无绝人之路……\n你知道我们历经了多少辛苦才到达这里吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:212`

### English

The alien refugees we once met, without the ability to communicate with one another, now stand before us on our screens.\n\n"Our peoples' paths merge again, [From.GetSpeciesAdj]. Now that we have finally found our new haven. The universe is almost cruel in its irony...\nDo you have any idea of the struggles we've been through to get here?"

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:214`

## anomaly.4093.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.a&lang=en)

### 中文

之前的事真的很抱歉！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Our apologies for earlier!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:215`

## anomaly.4093.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.b&lang=en)

### 中文

唯有自立，才能活下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Everyone needs to stand on their own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:216`

## anomaly.4093.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.c&lang=en)

### 中文

因为无法沟通，所以我们害怕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

With no way of communication we turned to fear.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:217`

## anomaly.4093.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4093.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你的生死与我们无关，异族。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Your survival is of no concern to us, alien.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:218`

## anomaly.4094.apology.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4094.apology.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们接受你的道歉，但要是当初你们帮助了我们，我们就不会死去那么多人了。再见了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

"Your apology is accepted, yet we can only imagine how many more of our kind would be here today had you helped us earlier. Farewell for now."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:219`

## anomaly.4094.insult.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4094.insult.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“说得轻巧，[From.GetSpeciesAdj]。尤其是对你这种站着说话不腰疼的人来说。继续袖手旁观吧，然后你便会孤立无援。再见了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

"That is an easy stance to take, [From.GetSpeciesAdj]. Especially when you are the one standing firmly with both feet on the ground. So stand proud, as you will most assuredly stand alone. Farewell."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:220`

## anomaly.4094.neutral.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4094.neutral.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“可以理解，[From.GetSpeciesAdj]。不过这对我们遭受到的痛苦毫无帮助。我们曾经在最需要帮助的时候孤苦无助。再见了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

"Understandable, [From.GetSpeciesAdj]. However it will not erase our pain, being left to survive in the great void without aid. Farewell."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:221`

## anomaly.4094.xenophobe.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4094.xenophobe.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“呵呵，[From.GetSpeciesAdj]。还好我们从一开始就看透了你们。再见了。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

"Right, [From.GetSpeciesAdj]. It is good to know that we had you sized up from the start. Farewell."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:222`

## anomaly.4094.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4094.a&lang=en)

### 中文

永别了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

Farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:223`

## anomaly.4095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

庇护所

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Sanctuary

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:224`

## anomaly.4095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的森林让船员们大吃一惊。这不仅仅是一个充斥着各种有机生物的高度发展的世界，而且大部分植物的根茎都能吃。或许不算美味，但这代表我们可以轻易地在[From.GetPlanetMoon]的肥沃土壤上耕种。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

The forests of [From.GetName] have blown the crew's expectations away. It is not only a highly developed world rich in organic life, but most plant roots are even edible. This might not sound appetizing, but it indicates that we could easily grow food on this [From.GetPlanetMoon], due to the rich soil.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:225`

## anomaly.4095.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4095.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§G青葱行星§!修正到[From.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

Adds the §GLush Planet§! modifier to [From.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:226`

## anomaly.4100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

水中王者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

The Rulers of the Water Plains

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:227`

## anomaly.4100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4100.base.desc$ 这些生物的暗色鳞片非常坚硬，而且在陌生人靠近时极易受惊。\n\n也许可以驯服这些猛兽，让它们协助我们将来的行星殖民活动。但先研究它们也许会有所收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

$anomaly.4100.base.desc$ The dark scales of the creatures are rock hard, and they are very skittish when approached.\n\nIt might be possible to domesticate the beasts and have them assist in potential future colonization of the planet. However, we might however happen across some interesting revelations by studying them first.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:228`

## anomaly.4100.expert.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.expert.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4100.base.desc$\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]发现这种动物有某种灵能能力，从它们的行为变化上看，它们可以感应到我们的意图。\n\n也许可以驯服这些猛兽，让它们协助我们将来的行星殖民活动。但先研究它们也许会有所收获。\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName]说这些当地生物会“对不怀好意的人反馈负面回应”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

$anomaly.4100.base.desc$\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] have been able to deduce that the animals have some sort of psionic ability, as changes in their demeanor indicates that they are able to intuitively sense our intentions.\n\nIt might be possible to domesticate the beasts and have them assist in potential future colonization of the planet. However, we might however happen across some interesting revelations by studying them first.\n\n[Root.GetLeaderName] claims that the indigenous creatures seem to "respond negatively to thoughts that indicate any sort of threat."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:229`

## anomaly.4100.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]上，科学官[Root.GetLeaderName]发现了一种身体健壮、适应力极强的四足哺乳生物。他们可以徒步涉水，即使是高大的植物也仅仅达到它们的腿部。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

On [From.GetName], Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has discovered a race of quadruped mammalians who appear very strong and physically resilient. They run across shallow plains of water, with lush plants reaching for their legs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:230`

## anomaly.4100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

研究它们，找出更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

Study them to find out more.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:231`

## anomaly.4100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

试图立刻驯服它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

Try to domesticate them right away.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:232`

## anomaly.4100.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4100.c&lang=en)

### 中文

让它们自由自在地生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Let them roam free.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:233`

## anomaly.4101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图靠近它们做研究，但进展并不顺利。它们一发现我们靠近，就要么逃走要么战斗，它们好像从大老远就能发现我们。\n\n这些动物明显清楚我们的意图，所以尽量避免跟我们接触。\n\n至少我们可以从远距离研究它们，也许会有所发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

Our attempts to study the animals up close has yielded few results. They run away or fight back as soon as we approach, as if they knew we are coming way before we have even sighted their herds.\n\nThe beasts clearly understand what we are trying to do, somehow, and avoid being in close proximity to us.\n\nAt least we can keep studying them from afar and maybe learn something interesting.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:234`

## anomaly.4101.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4101.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这最起码是一种意志坚定的动物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

A strong-willed animal to say the least.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:235`

## anomaly.4102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

接触这些动物的进展相当顺利！它们似乎拥有一种灵能感应，可以通过精神彼此沟通以及跟我们沟通。\n\n它们想要自由自在地生活在大地上，尽情享受那里的水果。如果我们不打扰它们的生存空间和食物，它们愿意在必要时帮助我们。\n\n这段佳话应当被广泛传播到人们的耳中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

Approaching the animals have yielded results! They appear to have psionic capabilities, able to communicate with each other, and us, via telepathy.\n\nThey wish to be free, roam the land and eat of its fruits. We would have their support, as fellow creatures of the cosmos, if we simply allow them their space and leave their food sources untouched.\n\nPositive stories of how this situation was resolved is bound to spread to the general populace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:236`

## anomaly.4102.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4102.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们自己也需要食物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

We need the food for ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:237`

## anomaly.4103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些动物非常难以驯服。为了靠近它们，我们被迫使用了暴力和麻醉剂。我们要驱使它们的时候，它们总是反抗。我们必须强迫它们完成工作，但效果不太好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

The animals are proving difficult to tame. In order to get even close to them we have to use some force and eventually sedate them. They consistently fight back when we try to put them to work. We will have to force them to perform their tasks, which hasn't turned out to be very efficient.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:238`

## anomaly.4103.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4103.a&lang=en)

### 中文

它们为什么要反抗呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

Wonder why they resist so?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:239`

## anomaly.4104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些动物非常难以相处。虽然我们已经尽可能地温柔了，但其中一部分还是逃走和反抗。我们聚集了比较温驯的一批，训练它们工作。但其中一部分还是抗拒了。我们现在有一小群个体愿意帮忙我们，但这相比整支族群明显低效了很多。\n\n我们驯服这些动物的消息一旦传入大众耳中，可能会对我们的声誉造成负面影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

The animals have been difficult to work with. While we have been as gentle as possible in our advance, some of them still flee and resist. We have gathered the calmer specimens and made them work the earth for tests. Some rejected that too. We now have a small selection of individuals that are willing to help us, but they will not be as effective as the entire herd.\n\nThe way we enslaved these animals may paint us in a bad light, once word spreads to the general populace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:240`

## anomaly.4104.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4104.a&lang=en)

### 中文

还是有用的嘛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

Always something.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:241`

## anomaly.4105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

驱之不散

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

The Fumes Lie Thick

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:242`

## anomaly.4105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]行星是一片有毒废土，但是我们的扫描发现这里曾经不是现在这样的。一个如今已毁灭的文明因为过度依赖改变气候的燃料和毒素而彻底毁了自己的这个母星。\n\n在一个大城市附近的一处偏僻山丘上，我们发现了一座还能运行的设施。这是一个组装机器人的工厂，周围泊满了军用车辆。但这些车辆稍稍一碰就化成了尘埃。\n\n这座设施非常神秘奇特，但也许能够提供不少研究材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

The planet [From.GetName] is a toxic wasteland, but our scans unveil that it has not always been so. A now extinct civilization seems to have destroyed its home planet, via a heavy reliance on climate altering fuels and toxins.\n\nOn a remote hill, close to a major city, we have found a lone active facility. A robot assembly plant, desolated and surrounded by military vehicles, so deteriorated they break upon touch.\n\nThe facility is alien and mysterious, but could provide us with ample study material.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:243`

## anomaly.4105.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4105.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]星球是一片剧毒而有腐蚀性的废土，但我们的扫描发现它并非一直如此。一个现已灭绝的有机体文明似乎因重度依赖改变气候的燃料与毒素，而摧毁了自己的母星。\n\n在靠近一座大型城市的偏远的山丘上，我们发现了一个尚在运行的设施。这是一个荒弃的机器人组装工厂，周围泊满了严重腐朽的军用车辆，稍稍一碰就化成了尘埃。\n\n这座设施非常神秘奇特，但也许能够提供不少研究材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

The planet [From.GetName] is a toxic, corrosive wasteland, but our scans unveil that it has not always been so. A now extinct organic civilization seems to have destroyed its home planet, via a heavy reliance on climate altering fuels and toxins.\n\nOn a remote hill, close to a major city, we have found a lone active facility. A robot assembly plant, desolated and surrounded by military vehicles, so deteriorated they break upon touch.\n\nThe facility is alien and mysterious, but could provide us with ample study material.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:244`

Adds the §YFallen Robotic Society§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+6§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:245`

### 中文

可引以为戒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

A cautionary tale.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:246`

## anomaly.4105.a.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4105.a.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

短视的有机生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

Organics are often short-sighted.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:247`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_renewable_energy$§! Empire Edict.\n\nAdds the §YFallen Robotic Society§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+6§!

## anomaly.4105.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4105.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

试图启动机器人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

Try and activate the robots.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:249`

## anomaly.4106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]上的那座设施因为科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]的努力而恢复运作了。\n\n机器人们启动后马上各就各位工作。[From.GetName]的船员们目瞪口呆，看着机器人们向四面八方移动，开始清除整颗星球上的毒素。\n\n这不禁让人猜测之前的居住者是因为过度关注于自相残杀而忽略了自救。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

The facility on [From.From.From.GetName] lights up as Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s plan finally comes to fruition.\n\nThe robots activate and immediately move with purpose. The crew of the [From.GetName] stare in shock, but warrantedly so, as the machines move out across the world, starting to cleanse the toxicity from it.\n\nOne could assume that the previous inhabitants were too wrapped up in conflict to save themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:250`

## anomaly.4106.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4106.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]上的那座设施因为科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]的努力而恢复运作了。\n\n机器人们启动后马上各就各位工作。[From.GetName]的船员单位们观察着这些古老的机器人向四面八方移动，开始清除整颗星球上的毒素。\n\n这不禁让人猜测之前的居住者是因为过度关注于自相残杀而忽略了自救。正是有机体一贯的作风。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

The facility on [From.From.From.GetName] lights up as Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s plan comes to fruition.\n\nThe robots activate and immediately move with purpose. The crew units of the [From.GetName] observe as the ancient robots move out across the world, starting to cleanse the toxicity from it.\n\nOne could assume that the previous inhabitants were too wrapped up in petty conflict to save themselves. How typical of organics.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:251`

## anomaly.4106.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4106.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]的§Y行星类型§!更改为§G大陆§!。\n\n添加§Y失落的机械社会§!修正到[From.From.From.GetName]：\n£engineering£ §G+3§!\n£building£ 机器人组装工厂 §G+1§!\n£blocker£ 坑 §R+2§!\n£blocker£ 城市废墟 §R+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

The §YPlanet Class§! of [From.From.From.GetName] changes to §GContinental§!.\n\nAdds the §YFallen Robotic Society§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+3§!\n£building£ Robotic Assembly Plant §G+1§!\n£blocker£ Craters §R+2§!\n£blocker£ City Ruins §R+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:252`

## anomaly.4106.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4106.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

解锁§Y$edict_renewable_energy$§!帝国法令。\n\n[From.From.From.GetName]的§Y行星类型§!更改为§G陆地§!。\n\n添加§Y失落的机器人社会§!修正到[From.From.From.GetName]：\n£engineering£ §G+3§!\n£building£ 机器人组装工厂 §G+1§!\n£blocker£ 弹坑 §R+2§!\n£blocker£ 城市废墟 §R+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_renewable_energy$§! Empire Edict.\n\nThe §YPlanet Class§! of [From.From.From.GetName] changes to §GContinental§!.\n\nAdds the §YFallen Robotic Society§! modifier to [From.From.From.GetName]:\n£engineering£ §G+3§!\n£building£ Robotic Assembly Plant §G+1§!\n£blocker£ Craters §R+2§!\n£blocker£ City Ruins §R+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:253`

## anomaly.4110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

流沙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

Shifting Sands

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:254`

## anomaly.4110.success.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4110.success.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的沙丘危险重重。那些光滑细腻的细沙就像液体般，对着陆造成了不可估量的困难。\n\n不过，我们机智的科学团队想到了一个创新的方法。由科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]配制的一种化学溶剂可以凝固关键地点的沙堆，让我们更容易地穿越[From.GetPlanetMoon]表面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

The sand dunes of [From.GetName] are treacherous indeed. The fine grained sand runs almost like a liquid, making most landings an ordeal beyond measure.\n\nHowever, our brilliant science team has found a solution thanks to some innovative thinking. A chemical solution, fabricated by Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName], can be used to bind the sand in key locations, making the [From.GetPlanetMoon] easier to traverse.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:255`

## anomaly.4110.fail.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4110.fail.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的沙丘危险重重。这些沙粒如此的细小，我们几乎可以将其完全看作液体，它会吞没一切踏足其上者。在这里殖民只会徒然地损失人力和设备。\n\n也许随着时间推移，我们会学会如何适应这样的环境。有句老话说得好：“站在前人的地基上。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

The sand dunes of [From.GetName] are treachearous indeed. The sand is so finely grained that it almost could be called a liquid, and it swallows anything that sets down upon it. Settling here would take a heavy toll on personnel as well as structures.\n\nWith time we could probably learn how to work around these conditions. As the saying went: "Eventually our castle would stand upon the old one."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:256`

## anomaly.4110.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4110.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y流沙§!修正到[From.GetName]：\n物理学研究§G+6§!\n£blocker£流沙盆地§R+5§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

Adds the §YLiquid Sands§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\nPhysics Research §G+6§!\n£blocker£ Quicksand Basin §R+5§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:257`

## anomaly.4110.b.tooltip

添加§Y移动的沙§!和§Y流沙§!修正到[From.GetName]：\n£physics£ §G+3§!\n£blocker£ 流沙盆地 §R+7§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

Adds the §YShifting Sands§! and §YLiquid Sands§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£physics£ §G+3§!\n£blocker£ Quicksand Basin §R+7§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:258`

## anomaly.4115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暗中蛰伏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

Lurking in the Dark

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:259`

## anomaly.4115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]出大事了！研究结束后并无重大发现，船员便返回到船上，却发现[From.GetPlanetMoon]的某只猛兽竟然跑上了船！\n\n该生物已经杀害了一位船员，并正在撕毁墙壁试图逼近其他的船员们。他们撑不了多久！已经发布一个§S特殊项目§!去救人了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

Catastrophe has struck the [Root.GetName]! After conducting their studies, with no interesting finds, the crew returned to the ship only to find it carrying a beast from the [From.GetPlanetMoon]! \n\nThe creature has already killed a crew member and is trying to claw itself through the walls to get to the others. They won't last long at this rate! A §SSpecial Project§! has been issued to save the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:260`

## anomaly.4115.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4115.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]发生了大灾难！在进行研究而一无所获之后，船员们返回飞船时却发现上面有一只来自[From.GetPlanetMoon]的有机体猛兽！\n\n这只猛兽已经瘫痪了一个船员单位，且正在撕毁墙壁试图逼近其他的单位。这样下去他们活不长了！拯救舰船的§S特殊项目§!已经发布。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

Catastrophe has struck the [Root.GetName]! After conducting their studies, with no interesting finds, the crew returned to the ship only to find it carrying an organic beast from the [From.GetPlanetMoon]! \n\nThe creature has already rendered one crew member unit inoperable, and is trying to claw itself through the walls to get to the others. They won't last long at this rate! A §SSpecial Project§! has been issued to save the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:261`

## anomaly.4115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

快组织救援队！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

Assemble a rescue force!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:262`

## anomaly.4115.b

## anomaly.4116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的救援队成功击毙了[From.From.GetName]上的那只猛兽。科学专员[From.From.GetLeaderName]和船员们都受到了惊吓，但依然能够胜任各自的职务。他们非常感激士兵们的援救，这件事激发了人们参军的热情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

Our rescue force has been successful in gunning down the beast aboard the [From.From.GetName]. Science Officer [From.From.GetLeaderName] and the team are rather traumatized, but assure that they are still fit for duty. Their appreciation of the soldiers saving them goes viral and we can already see a boost in new enlistees.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:265`

## anomaly.4116.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4116.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的救援队成功地消灭了[From.From.GetName]上的猛兽。科学专员[From.From.GetLeaderName]及其团队只受到了轻微的损伤，并仍可执行任务。武装单元成功排除有机威胁的行动引起了机械合成工厂对于机械安全的广泛重视。\n\n因此，我们的作战单元的建造速度以及动机软件的有效性已有所提高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

Our rescue force has been successful in gunning down the beast aboard the [From.From.GetName]. Science Officer [From.From.GetLeaderName] and the team have sustained negligible damage, and are still fit for duty. The success of our armed units in dispatching the organic menace has roused renewed focus among the assembly yards responsible for their construction.\n\nAs a result, we are noting an increase in combat unit build-speed and motivation software effectuality.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:266`

## anomaly.4116.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4116.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是舰队的骄傲！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

A proud day for the navy!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:267`

## anomaly.4116.a.tooltip

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$army_inspiration$§! modifier.

## anomaly.4116.a.mach.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4116.a.mach.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得§G$army_inspiration_mach$§!修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$army_inspiration_mach$§! modifier.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:269`

## anomaly.4117.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4117.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的救援队成功击毙了[From.From.GetName]上的那只猛兽。可惜他们没能及时拯救科学专员[From.From.GetLeaderName]，后者为了保护船员们而阵亡了。\n\n至少飞船无损，船员们很快就可以恢复工作。我们的母星沸腾了，都为我们的士兵们的英勇行为点赞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Our rescue force has been successful in gunning down the beast aboard the [From.From.GetName]. Sadly they didn't make it in time to save Science Officer [From.From.GetLeaderName] who died while trying to protect the crew.\n\nAt least the ship is intact and the crew says they can go back to work soon. Our homeworld becomes abuzz about our military's bravery in handling the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:270`

## anomaly.4117.desc.mach

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4117.desc.mach&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的救援队成功地消灭了[From.From.GetName]上的猛兽。不幸的是，他们未能及时拯救试图保护船员而受到了致命损伤的科学专员[From.From.GetLeaderName]。\n\n至少舰船依旧完好无损，而船上其它单位表示他们很快就能恢复正常机能。我们母星主控电路正忙于升级军事相关算法以更好的应对未来可能发生的类似事件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

Our rescue force has been successful in gunning down the beast aboard the [From.From.GetName]. Unfortunately, they didn't make it in time to save Science Officer [From.From.GetLeaderName], who took terminal damage while trying to protect the crew units.\n\nAt least the ship is intact and the crew informs us they can resume normal functions soon. Our homeworld's circuits are abuzz with updates on our military's efficacy in handling the situation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:271`

## anomaly.4117.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4117.a.tooltip&lang=en)
