失去舰船上的§R科学家§!。\n§Y你的帝国§!获得了§G$army_inspiration$§!修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Lose the ship's §RScience Leader§!.\n§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$army_inspiration$§! modifier.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:272`

## anomaly.4117.a.mach.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4117.a.mach.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

失去舰船上的§R科学家§!。\n§Y你的帝国§!获得了§G$army_inspiration_mach$§!修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Lose the ship's §RScience Leader§!.\n§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$army_inspiration_mach$§! modifier.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:273`

## anomaly.4120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天际巨响

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

Explosions in the Sky

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:274`

## anomaly.4120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]盛产大自然的奇迹。巨大的七彩爆炸出现在空中，这是因为大气层中的化学成分跟恒星的伽马射线产生了反应。\n\n学生们和高级科学家们都在传播着这个消息，消息如野火般扩散开来。科学领域中的相关人员都对此产生了兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

The [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName] is the home of a marvel of nature. Giant colorful explosions appear in the sky, a result of the chemical composition of the atmosphere reacting to the sun's gamma rays.\n\nStudents and senior scientists alike are spreading this find like a wildfire. It has generated quite the spark of interest for the scientific field amongst our people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:275`

## anomaly.4125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

熄灯的开采站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:274`

### English

Lights Out in the Mines

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:276`

## anomaly.4125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[From.GetPlanetMoon][From.GetName]的轨道中发现了一个漂浮着的旧开采站。可以确定它是用于开采行星资源的，但是主人是谁就是个迷了。\n\n开采站的科技水平跟我们很相似。我们应该很快能修复好它并让它重新运作。或者也可以拆毁它获取零件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

We have found an old mining station around the [From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.GetName], drifting in orbit. It was surely used to mine the planet of its resources, but by whom remains a mystery.\n\nThe technology within the station is very close to our own. We should be able to take hold of the station and have it up and running in no time. Or we could always scrap it for parts.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:277`

## anomaly.4125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

接管开采站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:276`

### English

Take control of the station.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:278`

## anomaly.4125.zerog.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.zerog.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4125.desc$\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]对于零重力建筑的博学多才让我们明白了开采站的内部构造，并且有助于改善我自己的设计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

$anomaly.4125.desc$\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName]'s superior knowledge of zero-g structures has helped us understand the inner workings of the station, allowing us to improve on our own designs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:279`

## anomaly.4125.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

添加§Y被遗弃的开采站§!修正到[From.GetName]：\n获得一个§Y开采站§!\n£energy£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

Adds the §YAbandoned Mining Facility§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\nGain a §YMining Station§!\n£energy£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:280`

## anomaly.4125.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4125.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§G零重力创新§!修正。\n\n添加§Y被遗弃的开采站§!修正到[From.GetName]：\n获得一个§Y开采站§!\n£energy£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §GZero-G Innovations§! modifier.\n\nAdds the §YAbandoned Mining Facility§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\nGain a §YMining Station§!\n£energy£ §G+3§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:281`

## anomaly.4130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大树

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

The Tree

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:282`

## anomaly.4130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]周围的小行星带，一棵大树在空旷的太空中漂浮着。这样空前未有的事情真是说出来谁都不敢信！大树被一个保护层般的泡泡包围着，看上去像个能源护盾。\n\n我们突然检测到敌对的太空种群正在接近！这恰巧发生在我们采集样本并有树液从护盾中流出来的时候，难道它们是被这东西吸引过来的？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

Among the asteroids around [From.GetName] a tree is drifting through the emptiness of space. An unprecedented and highly unlikely event! The tree is surrounded in a protective bubble, reminiscent of an energy shield.\n\nSuddenly we detect some hostile space dwellers on approach! This coincided with some sap leaking through the trees shield while we collected samples. Could it have agitated the creatures?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:283`

## anomaly.4130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得查出到底发生什么事了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

We need to figure out what is going on!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:284`

## anomaly.4131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过仔细的研究我们终于明白为什么树液会把那些生物吸引过来了。这棵树会生产一种非常高营养价值的液体，可以修复细胞组织。这简直是生命之树啊！\n\n如果我们可以采集这东西，我们就能够延长人们的寿命。但问题是：大树就一棵而已，我们要么大幅提升一小群人的寿命，要么小幅提升所有人的寿命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

After rigorous research we now understand why the tree's sap summoned those creatures. The tree is able to produce a highly nutritious substance, which has machine-like abilities to repair organic tissue. The tree is a tree of life!\n\nIf we harvested it we could use it to expand the lifespan of our people. But there is one problem: the tree's healing power is limited in amount. We could only use it to improve a small group of peoples' life substantially, or increase everyone's situation by a small amount.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:285`

## anomaly.4131.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4131.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拿去给领袖享用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:284`

### English

Give it to our leaders.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:286`

## anomaly.4131.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4131.b&lang=en)

### 中文

人人平等，大家一起用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

Give it to the people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:287`

## anomaly.4131.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4131.c&lang=en)

### 中文

让大树继续飘啊飘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

Let the tree drift along.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:288`

## anomaly.4135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

殖民者的教诲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

Teachings of Settlers

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:289`

## anomaly.4135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员在[From.GetName]上有重大发现。该行星上的沙丘隐藏着一些废墟，是属于一个星际种群的。这类殖民地大都敌不过时间的洗礼。\n\n科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]很快发现了一个藏有珍贵物品和文件的储物箱。既然这些文件是跟珍贵物品保存在一起，那一定相当重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

On [From.GetName] the crew of the [Root.GetName] have found something extraordinary. The planet's dunes have hid several ruins, belonging to an interstellar civilization of settlers. The settlements have long been destroyed by the ravages of time.\n\nScience Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is quick to discover a stored cache of valuables and documents. The collection of texts must have been most revered by the settlers, as they were stored amongst other valuables.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:290`

## anomaly.4135.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4135.b&lang=en)

### 中文

卖给私人收藏家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Sell it to a private collector.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:291`

## anomaly.4136.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4136.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的语言专家废寝忘食数个月，终于成功翻译了[From.From.From.GetName]上找到的文献。这些文件是一位被称为说书人特丽莎的著作。她似乎是位非常有名的哲学家、社会学家、关系学家。\n\n$anomaly.4136.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:290`

### English

Having had our linguistics experts hard at work for months, we've finally succeeded in translating the works found on [From.From.From.GetName].The papers are the teachings of an alien scholar only referred to as "Telisa the Teller of Tales." All mentions of her points to her being a famous philosopher and overall expert on society and relations.\n\n$anomaly.4136.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:292`

## anomaly.4136.found.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4136.found.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]上发现的文件似乎也是说书人特丽莎的著作。$anomaly.4136.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

It seems that the text we found on [From.From.From.GetName] is another piece created by "Telisa the Teller of Tales."$anomaly.4136.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:293`

## anomaly.4136.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4136.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些文件详细地解释如何重整基础设施和塑造社会。她的意思是所有人都牺牲自己的一部分幸福，为帝国共创美好的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

The documents explicitly details how you can refocus infrastructure and shape society. She meant that by having everyone sacrifice some of their comfort for the greater good the nation will flourish.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:294`

## anomaly.4136.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4136.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

可以使用§Y$edict_masters_writings_politics$§!的帝国法令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_masters_writings_politics$§! Empire Edict.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:295`

## anomaly.4140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

探索者的教诲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

### English

Teachings of Explorers

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:296`

## anomaly.4140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员发来报告称，他们在[From.GetName]上找到了惊人的发现。其他文明的探索队在其表面留下了痕迹，并遗留了一些保存完好的档案及文物。\n\n首席科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]提议将文献送回母星破译。但也可以将其卖给私人投资者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:295`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s crew is reporting that they've found something spectacular on [From.GetName]. On the surface lies traces of an explorative expedition of another civilization. Left behind are some well-preserved documents and artifacts.\n\nThe proposed action by chief Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is to send the text findings to our homeworld for translation. There is also the possibility to sell them off to a private investor.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:297`

## anomaly.4141.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4141.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过数周的研究，我们的语言学专家破解了[From.From.From.GetName]的文档中包含的内容。该文档记录了一位知名学者的哲学思辨，她被称为“说书人特丽莎”。\n\n$anomaly.4141.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

After many weeks of study, our expert linguists have unearthed the meaning behind the documents found on [From.From.From.GetName]. The documents were philosophical ponderings by a famous scholar, only referred to as "Telisa the Teller of Tales."\n\n$anomaly.4141.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:298`

## anomaly.4141.found.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4141.found.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谁能想到呢？我们发现的文献记录了更多“说书人特丽莎”的教诲。这次，是一些对更宏观问题的哲思与分析。\n\n$anomaly.4141.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

Who would've thought? The texts we found were more teachings from "Telisa the Teller of Tales." This time it's a collection of philosophical ponderings and analysis on larger questions.\n\n$anomaly.4141.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:299`

## anomaly.4141.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4141.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

她的教诲让我们对治理帝国有了更加深刻的理解，通过应用不同的看法，并在革命时关注宏观问题，可以获得更好的治理成果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Her teachings have provided us with much insight into how to better gain political results via applying different states of mind and use larger scale questions as a focus for reform.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:300`

## anomaly.4141.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4141.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

可以使用§Y$edict_masters_writings_philosophy$§!的帝国法令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_masters_writings_philosophy$§! Empire Edict.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:301`

## anomaly.4145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶主的毁灭

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

The Slavers' Downfall

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:302`

## anomaly.4145.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的核冬天还会持续很久。整个行星都没有生物存活。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报了队伍的调查结果：一切证据都指明，这里曾存在统一的文明，但陷入了争斗。\n\n战争的起因可能是一些小族长成为争夺帝国更大的奴隶控制权产生了矛盾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

On [From.GetName] the nuclear winter is far from over. Nothing alive remains on any part of the planet. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports the team's findings: Everything points towards a civil war having broken out within a previously unified civilization.\n\nThe root cause of the war was most likely the conflict between different stewards who all wanted control over larger parts of the union's slaves.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:303`

## anomaly.4145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\n所幸，我们的历史上没有发生这种事，但我们可以从中获得警示，施行奴隶制是一种愚傻的行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\nThankfully we're not close to this happening to us, but we should let it stand as a testament to the lunacy of slavery.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:304`

## anomaly.4145.slaver.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.slaver.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\n这些发现对我们的生活方式毫无帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:303`

### English

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\nThis does not help our own way of life appear in any sort of favorable light.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:305`

## anomaly.4145.soon.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.soon.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\n我们必须谨慎，免得步入后尘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:304`

### English

$anomaly.4145.base.desc$\n\nWe should think twice before treading the same path.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:306`

## anomaly.4145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

愚傻的奴隶主……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:305`

### English

The folly of slavers...

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:307`

## anomaly.4145.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.b&lang=en)

### 中文

真让人吃惊……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

One starts to wonder...

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:308`

## anomaly.4145.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§R$slavery_bad_juju$§!修正。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §R$slavery_bad_juju$§! modifier.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:309`

## anomaly.4145.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4145.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得了§R$slavery_very_bad_juju$§!修正。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §R$slavery_very_bad_juju$§! modifier.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:310`

## anomaly.4150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

贸易商的教诲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

Teachings of Traders

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:311`

## anomaly.4150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]的表面屹立着值得注意的建筑群。这些遗迹拥有不同的建筑风格，但都建于同一时代。[From.GetPlanetMoon]曾经可能是一个交易中心。\n\n某些遗迹中存有文档和财物。我们可以通过研究文本来尝试翻译，以便了解曾定居在这里的生物。我们也可以将其卖给古董收藏家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

The surface of [From.GetName] holds something remarkable. Multiple ruins can be found, built using different architectures, yet from the same point in history. The [From.GetPlanetMoon] might have been a trading hub!\n\nSome ruins held stored documents and valuables. If we studied the texts we might be able to translate them and learn more about the people who settled here. Or we could sell them to someone who values old heirlooms.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:312`

## anomaly.4151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们天才的语言学专家正在努力翻译[From.From.From.GetName]上发现的文献。文献的作者被称作“说书人特丽莎”，似乎是当时的德高望重之人。\n\n$anomaly.4151.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

Our most talented linguists have been hard at work in translating the documents we found on [From.From.From.GetName]. The scribe behind these works is referred to as "Telisa the Teller of Tales," and it seems she was highly revered during her time.\n\n$anomaly.4151.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:313`

## anomaly.4151.found.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4151.found.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谁能想到呢？我们发现的文献记录了更多“说书人特丽莎”的教诲。\n\n$anomaly.4151.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

Who would've thought? The texts we found were more teachings from "Telisa the Teller of Tales."\n\n$anomaly.4151.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:314`

## anomaly.4151.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4151.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这份文档记录了一些奇闻趣事、如何从星际外交中获取最大利益的建议和技巧。她相信，公平贸易和互相尊重的沟通可以为各界带来巨大的社会进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

The documents we found were a collection of anecdotes, suggestions and techniques for how to optimally partake in interstellar diplomacy. She seemed to have believed that good trade and respectful communication would achieve great social advancements for all parties.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:315`

## anomaly.4151.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4151.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

可以使用§Y$edict_masters_writings_diplomacy$§!的帝国法令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_masters_writings_diplomacy$§! Empire Edict.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:316`

## anomaly.4155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战场之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Beneath the Battlefield

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:317`

## anomaly.4155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]曾经拥有一个高等文明。但他们早已成为历史尘埃。异形猛兽在星球表面的远古战场上肆虐横行。\n\n在石山地区的山脉底部藏有一个避难所。如果我们能先处理掉地表的猛兽，我们就可以继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

[From.GetName] used to hold an advanced civilization. It is now dead silent. Alien beasts roam old battlefields that stretch across the surface.\n\nIn a rocky region there seems to lie a hidden vault beneath the mountains. If we could get rid of the beasts roaming the landscape we could investigate it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:318`

## anomaly.4155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

马上动手尝试！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

Let's try and get to it!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:319`

## anomaly.4155.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4155.b&lang=en)

### 中文

远离避难所和在那里游荡的灵魂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

Leave the vault, and its wandering spirits be.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:320`

## anomaly.4156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

清除猛兽后，我们进入了避难所。内部似乎是一间指挥室，为这个死亡文明的领袖们提供最后的庇护。\n\n避难所内全是破损的设备和腐烂的尸体。唯一值得调查的是一个容器，可能装有十分重要的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

With the beasts removed, we could access the vault. Inside it lay a headquarter of sorts, a last refuge for a dying world's leaders.\n\nThe vault is filled with destroyed equipment and decomposed corpses. The only thing of interest being a container, which potentially holds something of importance.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:321`

## anomaly.4156.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4156.b&lang=en)

### 中文

以防万一，只研究避难所。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Only study the vault to avoid risks.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:322`

## anomaly.4157.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4157.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冷冻舱中的勇士

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

Cryopod Warrior

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:323`

## anomaly.4157.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4157.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[WarriorHomePlanet.GetName]上发现的容器原来是一个冷藏装置！封存的外星人已经苏醒。她用心灵感应说道：\n\n“为什么叫醒我？我们一族的战争结束了吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

The container found on [WarriorHomePlanet.GetName] turned out to be a cryogenic device! The preserved alien within has awoken. She speaks using telepathic concepts:\n\n"Why have you brought me back, captor? Is the war of my people over?"

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:324`

## anomaly.4157.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4157.a&lang=en)

### 中文

你目前在[HomePlanet.GetName]上。这里很安全。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

You are on [HomePlanet.GetName]. You are safe here.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:325`

## anomaly.4157.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4157.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你的家乡被战争毁灭了，只有你幸存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Your home planet was ruined by the war, only you remain.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:326`

## anomaly.4157.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4157.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尽力从你们被毁的家园抢救物资。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

We salvaged what we could of your ruined homeworld.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:327`

## anomaly.4158.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我就怕这种事发生。希望我的知识和我们种群的悲剧能带给别人教诲。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

"I feared that this would happen. I only hope that my knowledge, and our failure as a species, can teach others."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:328`

## anomaly.4159.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4159.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“是这样吗……我就怕醒来时会得到这个消息。说实话，我更怕永远醒不过来。你们一族想从我这里获得什么？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

"So that's why... I feared that I would wake up to these news. To be honest I feared I would never wake at all. What does your kind want of me?"

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:329`

## anomaly.4158.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.a&lang=en)

### 中文

和我们说说你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

Tell us about yourself.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:330`

## anomaly.4158.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

“我叫玛居拉，拥有‘领受者’的称号。我是一名将军，是东方女王的顾问，也是她忠诚的朋友。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

"I am Majula, the one bearing the title of "The Bearer." I was a general, advisor to the Queen of the East, and her loyal friend."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:331`

## anomaly.4158.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.b&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么你被冷藏了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:330`

### English

Why were you preserved?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:332`

## anomaly.4158.spiritual.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.spiritual.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你是出于什么神圣的任务而被冷藏的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Were you preserved for a divine mission?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:333`

## anomaly.4158.militant.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.militant.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在战时被冷冻，你一定是个重要人物？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

To be frozen in war you must be of high value?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:334`

## anomaly.4158.b.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.b.response&lang=en)

### 中文

“我拥有‘领受者’的称号。如果我们走向了灭亡，我要负责代表我的人民。除了对自己物种的了解，我在战争方面的知识最为丰富。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

"I bore the title of "The Bearer." I was responsible of representing our people, would we end up walking the path of extinction. My knowledge of war was only outranked by my knowledge of our people."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:335`

## anomaly.4158.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.c&lang=en)

### 中文

说说你的人民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Tell us about your people.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:336`

## anomaly.4158.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

“惹是生非的种群。我们的历史中……走向毁灭之路的故事比比皆是。人们不懂得实现自身价值。虽然存在许多明智之人，但很少有人愿意倾听。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

"A troubled kind. Our history is... was filled with stories of our inability to steer clear from the road to destruction. The group was unable to live up to the potential of the individual. We had many wise speakers, but few who chose to listen."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:337`

## anomaly.4158.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.d&lang=en)

### 中文

你会加入我们吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Will you join us?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:338`

## anomaly.4158.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.f&lang=en)

### 中文

离开吧。你的命运由你自己掌控。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Leave. Your fate is your own.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:339`

## anomaly.4158.xenophobe.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4158.xenophobe.f&lang=en)

### 中文

你没有利用价值。走！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

You are of no use to us. Leave!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:340`

## anomaly.4160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“你的话语和你的文明向我证明了一切，捕获者。我会加入你，希望你能吸取我们种群的教训。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

"Your words, and your civilization speaks to me, captor. I will join you, in hopes that you will learn from my people's mistakes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:341`

## anomaly.4161.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4161.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你的话语，还有这个房间的氛围，都证明了我们的观点不合。所以我不会加入你。我会回到[WarriorHomePlanet.GetName]，穷尽一生讲述它的故事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

The way you speak. The way this room speaks for you. It tells me that our views do not align, captor. So no, I will not join you. I will return to [WarriorHomePlanet.GetName], and tell its stories for as long as I remain."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:342`

## anomaly.4161.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4161.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就静静地离开吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

Then go in peace.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:343`

## anomaly.4161.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4161.b&lang=en)

### 中文

抓住她！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Seize her!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:344`

## anomaly.4162.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4162.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“宁死不屈！”\n\n玛居拉愤怒地冲出房间。外面的人群正准备进行阻拦，她就抓住了一名研究员作为人质。在逃离过程中，人质不幸牺牲而玛居拉也被警卫击毙。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

"I would rather die!"\n\nMajula runs out of the room, enraged. As a crowd had gathered outside to take part in the proceedings she grabs a researcher to use as leverage. In the escape, the researcher dies before Majula is gunned down by security.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:345`

## anomaly.4163.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4163.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“再见了，捕获者。试着吸取我们物种的教训，千万不要在我们下一次相遇时，你也成了你们物种的最后一人。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

"Farewell, captor. Try and learn from what happened to my people, lest we meet again and you too have ended up to be the last of your kind."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:346`

## anomaly.4163.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4163.a&lang=en)

### 中文

永别了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

Farewell.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:347`

## anomaly.4163.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4163.b&lang=en)

### 中文

出去！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

Get out!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:348`

## anomaly.4165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

勇士的教诲

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

Teachings of Warriors

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:349`

## anomaly.4165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[From.GetName]的荒野上，发现了战斗过的痕迹。在毫无生机的地表下，[Root.GetName]的研究员发现了骸骨和金属碎片，最令人吃惊的是一个装着文档的容器。\n\n如果我们将文档送回母星破译，我们有机会了解这场战争和参与战争的种群。但也可以直接将其卖给古董商。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

Across the barren fields of [From.GetName] there are indications of old battles being fought. Beneath the lifeless ground our researchers on the [Root.GetName] have found bones, metallic shards and, most surprisingly, a container filled with documents.\n\nIf we send the documents to our homeworld for translation we could potentially learn more about this war and who fought in it. It would also be possible to simply sell them off as antiques.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:350`

## anomaly.4166.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4166.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几周来，我们的语言学专家一直在破译远古战场留下的文档。今天研究有了一些进展。我们得知，倒下的勇士遵循的教义来自于一个叫“说书人特丽莎”的哲学家。\n\n$anomaly.4166.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

Our language experts have been at it for weeks to try and translate the documents we found in the old battlefield. Today we finally have some results. We now know that the fallen warriors all followed the teachings of a philosopher called "Telisa the Teller of Tales."\n\n$anomaly.4166.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:351`

## anomaly.4166.found.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4166.found.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从战场上挖掘到的文献上记录了更多“说书人特丽莎”的教诲。\n\n$anomaly.4166.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

It appears that the texts we found buried in the battlefield were more teachings written by "Telisa the Teller of Tales."\n\n$anomaly.4166.base.desc$

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:352`

## anomaly.4166.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4166.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个文档对各种战术、战略进行了解析，最关键的是，其中包含星际战争的后勤学。特丽莎本人强烈反对战争，但她认为这种知识在未来时代仍然十分重要。“为了创建更美好的世界，即便是最和平的种群，也会有被逼迫到死角的时候。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

The documents are breakdowns of tactics, strategy and, most importantly, logistics in interstellar warfare. Being a strong spokesperson against war, Telisa still found this to be essential knowledge for steering into the future. "Even the most peaceful of people might be forced into a corner in their work for a better world."

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:353`

## anomaly.4166.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4166.a&lang=en)

### 中文

看来智者也会选择战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Even the wise can go to war it seems.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:354`

## anomaly.4166.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4166.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

可以使用§Y$edict_masters_writings_war$§!的帝国法令。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

Gives access to the §Y$edict_masters_writings_war$§! Empire Edict.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:355`

## anomaly.4170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

易燃性资源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

Volatile Resources

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:356`

## anomaly.4170.start.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.start.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描结果显示，[From.GetPlanetMoon]的地表下拥有高浓度的天然气。这种丰厚的资源非常高效节能，可以大大增加地表设施的能源产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

Scans of the [From.GetPlanetMoon] has unveiled dense deposits of natural gases underneath the surface. The rich resource is extremely energy efficient and would greatly increase the energy output of our surface production.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:357`

## anomaly.4170.basic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.basic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4170.start.desc$\n\n然而，这些资源将会很快耗尽，因为储量稀少、体积较小。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

$anomaly.4170.start.desc$\n\nHowever, using these resources would deplete them rather quickly, as the deposits are rare and small in size.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:358`

## anomaly.4170.material.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.material.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly.4170.start.desc$\n\n它带来的好处远比对环境造成的破坏要大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

$anomaly.4170.start.desc$\n\nThe potential gain this would serve to us because heavily outweighs any damage that could be caused to the environment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:359`

## anomaly.4170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.a&lang=en)

### 中文

准备进行开采！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

Prepare to start digging!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:360`

## anomaly.4170.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]获得§Y丰富气藏§!的修正：\n£energy£ §G+50%§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

Adds the §YRich Gas Deposits§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£energy£ §G+50%§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:361`

## anomaly.4170.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.b&lang=en)

### 中文

开采行星不是长久之计。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

Exploiting the planet is not a long-term solution.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:362`

## anomaly.4170.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4170.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]获得§Y合理气体开采§!的修正：\n£energy£ §G+5§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

Adds the §YResponsible Gas Mining§! modifier to [From.GetName]:\n£energy£ §G+5§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:363`

## anomaly.4172.conqueror.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4172.conqueror.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们占据的殖民地曾拥有丰富的地下气体资源。但现在已经耗尽，工厂的能源输出降低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

The colony we took over used to hold rich resources of gas underneath the surface. Now they've depleted, reducing the energy output of our industries.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:364`

## anomaly.4172.owner.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly.4172.owner.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

富含能源的气体已经耗尽。这个殖民地已失去额外的资源输出，大大增加了我们对新矿藏的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

The energy rich gas found on one of our colonies has now depleted. The extra resource output has been lost, greatly increasing our need for new deposits to replace it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:365`

## anomaly_failure.4000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetLeaderName]有了惊人发现。[From.From.GetName]的植被对非植物有机体极其危险。我们还不清楚其中原理；只知道[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]十分危险，这将限制其殖民地的发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

[Root.GetLeaderName] has made a traumatizing discovery. The flora on [From.From.GetName] is extremely dangerous to non-plant organisms. We do not fully understand what is causing this; all we know is that large parts of the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon] is outright lethal and that this will restrict growth of potential settlements.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:370`

## anomaly_failure.4000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是个可怕的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

This is truly a horrific place.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:371`

## anomaly_failure.4000.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4000.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]获得§R$leader_trait_paranoid$§!特质。\n\n[From.From.GetName]获得§Y酸性丛林§!的特质：\n£blocker£ 剧毒沼泽 §R+3§!\n£society£ §G+6§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] gains the §R$leader_trait_paranoid$§! Trait.\n\nAdds the §YAcid Jungles§! modifier to [From.From.GetName]:\n£blocker£ Noxious Swamp §R+3§!\n£society£ §G+6§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:372`

## anomaly_failure.4005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很不幸，我们的科研队伍没能从小行星中发现任何有价值的东西，和当初预想的不同，只发现了一些普通矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Unfortunately, our team has not been able to deduce anything of great interest from the asteroid, as first expected, except for some common minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:373`

## anomaly_failure.4005.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4005.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetName]增添资源：\n£minerals£ §G+1§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Adds resources to [From.From.GetName]:\n£minerals£ §G+1§!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:374`

## anomaly_failure.4010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报称，经过大范围探测和扫描后，并未在[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]上发现有价值的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that after extensive probing and scanning of the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon], nothing of interest has been found.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:375`

## anomaly_failure.4011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在调查飞船和船员的残骸后，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]推测船员们一定是自相残杀。太空旅行的孤独与禁闭甚至可以击溃最强大的意志。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

Having searched the ship and inspected the crew's remains, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] has deduced that the crewmembers must have killed each other. The solitude and confinement of space travel can break even the strongest of minds.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:376`

## anomaly_failure.4012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[Root.GetName]收到了求救信号。船员开始发疯地互相残杀！舰长已经失去控制，派船救援已经来不及了。这和他们刚刚调查的古船有关吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

We are receiving a distress signal from the [Root.GetName]. The crew has gone mad and are killing each other! The captain has lost all control, sending help would be futile at this point. Could this have anything to do with that derelict ship they recently investigated?

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:377`

## anomaly_failure.4012.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4012.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.GetName]§!被摧毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

The §Y[Root.GetName]§! is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:378`

## anomaly_failure.4015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的船员汇报称，他们没有发现有价值的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

The [Root.GetName]'s crew report that they have found nothing of interest.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:379`

## anomaly_failure.4025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的科研队伍汇报称，他们没能按预期找到[From.From.GetName]上的珍贵数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

The science team of the [Root.GetName] reports that they have not found the impactful data they first expected [From.From.GetName] to hold.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:380`

## anomaly_failure.4030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

翻遍了整个沙漠，我们也未能找到有价值的东西！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Having combed the desert, we have not found anything of value!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:381`

## anomaly_failure.4031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们未能阻止[From.From.GetName]上的飞舰载人工智能。他们的古船飞向遥远的天际，按照程序参数寻找下一个战场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

We were unable to stall the fleet AI on [From.From.GetName]. Their ancient ships are taking to the skies and beyond, looking for the next battle according to their programmed parameters.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:382`

## anomaly_failure.4031.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4031.a&lang=en)

### 中文

机器的怒火值得恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

The rage of the machine is worth fearing.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:383`

## anomaly_failure.4035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报称，他们苦心寻找的辐射是突发的设备异常所造成的读数错误。现在已经修理好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the radiation readings they so desperately wanted to examine were the result of a rare instrument malfunction. It has now been taken care of.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:384`

## anomaly_failure.4037.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4037.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据辐射读数，这不是我们要寻找的恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

The radiation readings indicate that this is not the solar system we were looking for.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:385`

## anomaly_failure.4037.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4037.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

We have to keep looking.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:386`

## anomaly_failure.4045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们就是无法确定[From.From.GetName]上微弱读数的源头。失落的科研队表示会思过整改。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

We simply cannot lock down what those subtle readings on [From.From.GetName] were. The team is frustrated and promise to improve.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:387`

## anomaly_failure.4055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]汇报称，他们未能找到原本承诺会找到的东西。科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]因此心烦意乱，并为失误道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

The [Root.GetName] reports that they have been unable to find anything to deliver on the promised finds. Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] is distraught and apologizes for the mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:388`

## anomaly_failure.4055.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4055.a&lang=en)

### 中文

别担心，[Root.GetLeaderName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

Do not worry, [Root.GetLeaderName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:389`

## anomaly_failure.4055.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4055.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不要再犯错了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

Do not make any more mistakes.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:390`

## anomaly_failure.4060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过仔细调查后，科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]只在恒星轨道上发现了普通的太空碎片。它们可能源自附近的小行星带。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:388`

### English

After closer inspection, Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] only found random space debris orbiting the star. It probably originated from a nearby asteroid belt.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:391`

## anomaly_failure.4066.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4066.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一切都太迟了，我们未能阻止前进的钻头。它钻得太深，最后被重重的岩石压碎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:389`

### English

We could not stop the rampaging drill before it was too late. It dug too deep and was eventually crushed under the weight of rocky detritus of its own creation.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:392`

## anomaly_failure.4070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

科学专员[Root.GetLeaderName]汇报称，[From.From.GetPlanetMoon][From.From.GetName]遵循的轨道与普通热带[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]的预计轨道相同。这次异常报告似乎是误报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Science Officer [Root.GetLeaderName] reports that the [From.From.GetPlanetMoon] [From.From.GetName] follows the patterns expected of a tropical [From.From.GetPlanetMoon]. The anomaly that was detected seems to have been a dud.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:393`

## anomaly_failure.4071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们遇到了麻烦，和初步扫描结果不同，[From.From.GetName]其实十分危险。对其殖民可能会遭遇不利情况，如灾害性天气、高温和毒性动植物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Troublingly, we have found that [From.From.GetName] is very hostile, despite our initial scans pointing towards nothing of the sort. Potential colonizers would have to deal with terrible conditions, such as hazardous weathers, high temperatures and poisonous plants and wildlife.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:394`

## anomaly_failure.4075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

严峻的气候让我们损失惨重，研究小队没能发掘更多关于[From.GetName]的未知信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

The harsh climate has taken its toll and our science team have failed to reveal anything about [From.From.GetName] that we did not already know.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:395`

## anomaly_failure.4080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.GetPlanetMoon]似乎没有预想中的那么特别。先前的读数异常似乎是由偶然形成的对称矿脉造成的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:393`

### English

The [From.From.GetPlanetMoon] seems to be less interesting than we had hoped. The readings pointing to previous settlements seem to have been triggered by oddly symmetrical mineral formations.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:396`

## anomaly_failure.4081.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4081.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.From.From.GetName]的修复工程传来坏消息。建筑工人未能修理坏掉的机器，机器开始自行解体，已经无法修理了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

Bad news from the repairs project on [From.From.From.GetName]. The construction workers have not been able to fix the broken machine, as it has started to break down even further and seems to be beyond repair.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:397`

## anomaly_failure.4086.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4086.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

很遗憾，未能抢救靠近[From.From.From.GetName]的漂流船。一个未侦测到的小行星突然出现在碰撞航向上，把那艘船撞成了碎片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Sadly we were not able to save the drifting ship close to [From.From.From.GetName] before it was too late. An untracked asteroid appeared on a collision course and smashed the ship to pieces.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:398`

## anomaly_failure.4086.military

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4086.military&lang=en)

### 中文

上面的货物更重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

The cargo was more important.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:399`

## anomaly_failure.4090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们丢失了信号。那个信号似乎是一艘飞船发出的，但突然就消失了。也许它进行了超光速跃迁，或者遭遇了更可怕的状况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

We lost the signal. It appears to have come from a ship, but suddenly it just vanished. It might have performed an FTL jump, or something much worse happened to it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:400`

## anomaly_failure.4090.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4090.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一次新的接触机会就这么丢失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

A new contact, lost to the void.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:401`

## anomaly_failure.4090.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4090.b&lang=en)

### 中文

很好。我们并不关心外星人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

Good. Alien interests are none of our concern.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:402`

## anomaly_failure.4116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

被怪兽侵袭的飞船发来了最后的电报。那些野兽已经成功突破到了船员藏身的地方。\n\n情况太过残忍，就不多做描述了。没有生还者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

A last transmission has been received from the monster-infested ship. It tells us that the beast succeeded in getting through to the crew's hiding place.\n\nThe horrid encounter was far too graphic to describe. There were no survivors.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:403`

## anomaly_failure.4116.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4116.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们辜负了他们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:401`

### English

We failed them...

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:404`

## anomaly_failure.4116.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4116.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y科研船§!被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

The §YScience Ship§! is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:405`

## anomaly_failure.4136.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4136.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

十分可惜，我们的语言学家未能完全理解[From.From.From.GetName]上古老文献的外星语言。不过，他们翻译了一些核心内容，让我们得以窥探那个文明的文化。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:403`

### English

Sadly our linguists have been unable to understand the entirety of the alien language used in the old documents found on [From.From.From.GetName]. However, they managed to translate the essentials, which has given us a glimpse into the culture they originated from.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:406`

## anomaly_failure.4156.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4156.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[WarriorHomePlanet.GetName]的地下避难所附近清除野兽时，战斗意外地导致了建筑崩塌。我们未能从残骸中回收到有价值的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Fighting off the beasts around the underground vault on [WarriorHomePlanet.GetName] has caused the complex to collapse. We are unable to salvage anything of value from the debris.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:407`

## anomaly_failure.4157.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4157.base&lang=en)

### 中文

打开[WarriorHomePlanet.GetName]上发现的容器后，我们找到了一些健康作物的样本，还有一些辅助器材，这可以让样本在肥沃条件下重新生长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

Opening up the container we found on [WarriorHomePlanet.GetName] revealed it to hold samples of healthy crops, as well as instruments that would help it grow again in infertile conditions.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:408`

## anomaly_failure.4157.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4157.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly_failure.4157.base$我们可以用逆向工程技术研究它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

$anomaly_failure.4157.base$ We could reverse engineer this.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:409`

## anomaly_failure.4157.maxed.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4157.maxed.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly_failure.4157.base$.\n\n虽然意义不大，但这个文明的文化历史数据可以让我们更了解他们的真面目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:407`

### English

$anomaly_failure.4157.base$.\n\nWhile not being that useful to us, there were also data on the civilization's culture and history, which could give us more insight into who they were.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:410`

## anomaly_failure.4158.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4158.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

打开[WarriorHomePlanet.GetName]上发现的容器后，我们才意识到那是一个生物武器的发射器。\n\n这种生物武器专门降低目标的生殖能力，并且扩散迅速。不过和我们的遗传因子不兼容，所以不会影响我们种群的数量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:408`

### English

After opening the container we found on [WarriorHomePlanet.GetName] we were shocked to realize that it was a dispenser for a biological weapon!\n\nThe bio-weapon was made to target fertility functions and quickly spreads. It is however not entirely compatible with our DNA, and will not cause us to diminish in numbers.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:411`

## anomaly_failure.4158.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4158.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得§R抑制繁衍生物武器§!的修正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §RFertility Inhibiting Bio-Weapon§! modifier.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:412`

## anomaly_failure.4170.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4170.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]上的气体开采设施已经化为一片火海。据报道，那种高度易燃的气体遇到了明火，导致了许多设施的爆炸！经过一连串的连锁反应，几乎炸光了我们侦探到的所有气体矿藏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

The gas mining facility on [From.GetName] has turned into a burning inferno. The report says that the highly volatile gas was exposed an open flame, causing multiple facilities to explode! This in turn caused a chain reaction and blew up most of the gas caches we had located.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:413`

## anomaly_failure.4170.individ.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4170.individ.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$anomaly_failure.4170.base.desc$\n\n不能再犯同样的错误了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

$anomaly_failure.4170.base.desc$\n\nThis backlash cannot be allowed to be repeated.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:414`

## anomaly_failure.4170.basic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aanomaly_failure.4170.basic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

## colony.2001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天堂的烦恼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Trouble in Paradise

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:425`

## colony.2001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]本地的政府失误导致一小群叛军收获了更多支持者，他们反对我们的传统思潮。支持他们的人越来越多，这让他们在该区域获得了更大的权利。这样的支持同时也让他们在获得民众支持上取得了一定成功，他们可以利用这些支持来发展自己的事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Local political drama on [Root.GetName] has resulted in increased support among a small group of rebels, opposed to our underlying ethics. Their increased support have enabled them to gain more power in the region and vote for their own causes with limited success.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:426`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2001.a&lang=en)

和他们划清界限。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

We stand divided.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:427`

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:428`

## colony.2005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

波涛之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

From Beneath the Waves

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:434`

## colony.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了改善日常生活，[Root.GetName]的殖民者开始定期探索星球上的资源。最近一次北极探索时，在深海发现了一个巨大物体：一艘外星战列巡洋舰。\n\n深海的压强可能会造成困难，但仍有可能打捞这艘沉船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

The colonizers on [Root.GetName] regularly explore the planet in hopes of finding more resources to help with day-to-day life. A recent expedition to the north pole has now unveiled a large object on the bottom of the ocean: An alien battle cruiser.\n\nThe pressure of the deep will prove a challenge, but it is possible to get to the ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:435`

## colony.2005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试打捞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

Try and salvage it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:436`

## colony.2005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

拆解并研究其中的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Scrap it to learn its secrets.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:437`

## colony.2006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的打捞任务顺利进行！沉在海底的远古巡洋舰重新浮出水面，并已修理完毕。真是蔚为大观，通过修理它，我们的工程师学到了其设计背后的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

The salvage mission on [Root.GetName] has succeeded! The ancient cruiser that lay on the bottom of the ocean has been resurfaced and repaired. It is a wondrous sight to behold, and from repairing it our engineers have learnt some of the secrets behind its design.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:438`

## colony.2006.a.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2006.a.material&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的努力将引领我们向前！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

Our efforts pave the way forward!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:439`

## colony.2006.a.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2006.a.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

具有历史意义的一天！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

A historic day!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:440`

## colony.2006.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2006.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得§G$streamlined_ship_design$§!的修正。\n\n获得一艘§Y巡洋舰§!类型的飞船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$streamlined_ship_design$§! modifier.\n\nGain a §YCruiser§!-class ship.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:441`

## colony.2007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尝试从[Root.GetName]的深海打捞这艘巨型飞船，并对其舰体设计有了一定发现。该船的机动算法很容易改用到我们的硬件上，让我们的飞船更加灵活，更加节能。\n\n可惜的是，该船本身无法抢修了，但我们仍可以从其舰体上回收一些材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

The attempts to salvage the giant ship that was found in [Root.GetName]'s waters have yielded some insight into the ship's design. The ship's maneuvering algorithms are easily transferable to our own hardware, enabling our fleets to become swifter and more energy efficient.\n\nUnfortunately the ship itself is beyond saving, but its hull will supply us with enough materials to make up for that.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:442`

## colony.2007.a.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2007.a.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

反正外星飞船是无法和我们的飞船比拟的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

No alien ship would surpass ours anyway!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:443`

## colony.2007.a.phile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2007.a.phile&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙中任何种群留下的馈赠都是有价值的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

Any gift from cosmos' children is valuable!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:444`

## colony.2007.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2007.a&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y你的帝国§!获得§G$streamlined_ship_design$§!的修正。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

§YYour Empire§! gains the §G$streamlined_ship_design$§! modifier.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:445`

## colony.2008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

爆炸声从数里之外都听得清清楚楚，打捞沉船的行动以灾难性失败告终。[Root.GetName]的居民没有受到伤害，但一部分建筑遭到了破坏。\n\n沉船被炸得粉碎，已经无法修理。所幸，我们回收到了许多残片。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

The explosion can be heard for miles, as the salvage operation of the submerged cruiser ends in disaster. The people of [Root.GetName] are without harm, yet parts of the settlement are destroyed.\n\nThe ship itself is far beyond repair, ripped apart by the explosion. Luckily, many of the scattered pieces of the hull can be salvaged.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:446`

## colony.2008.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2008.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的一栋£building£建筑被§R摧毁§!。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

A £building£ Building on [Root.GetName] is §Rruined§!.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:447`

## colony.2010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

思维转变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

A Change of Heart

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:448`

## colony.2010.desc.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.desc.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们在[Root.GetName]扎根落地，某些居民开始偏离我们原本的道路。虽然这是意料之中的事，但他们已经拥有了不少的支持者。该群体认为，我们对宇宙唯心主义的世界观无法快速引领我们前进，而他们坚信更加唯物主义的宇宙观才是答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

As we have grown our roots on [Root.GetName] certain individuals have begun to stray from our beaten path. That is to be expected, but these have supporters. The group feels our spiritualistic view of the universe is not leading us forward fast enough - assured that a more materialistic view of the cosmos is the answer.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:449`

## colony.2010.desc.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.desc.material&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们在[Root.GetName]上的扩张逐步稳定，某些居民开始偏离我们原本的世界观。一群唯心主义者开始和平地传播思想以反对我们的政府。他们认为我们对宇宙唯物主义的观点正令我们作茧自缚，并让我们与灵魂渐行渐远。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

As our expansion on [Root.GetName] has stabilized, certain individuals have begun to move away from our view of the world. A group of spiritualists has begun to peacefully propagate against our government. They see that our materialistic view of the cosmos is limiting us and distancing us from the soul.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:450`

## colony.2010.a.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.a.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

任其发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

Leave them be to find their own way.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:451`
