## colony.2010.c.material.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.c.material.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

将会尝试劝阻[Root.GetName]上的£pop£§H唯心主义人口§!并且宣扬§Y唯物主义§!。\n约§Y300§!£pop£§H人口§!获得§Y唯心主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:458`

## colony.2010.c.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.c.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

让精神向导帮他们改邪归正。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

Have spiritual guides re-direct them.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:459`

## colony.2010.c.spirit.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2010.c.spirit.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

将会尝试劝阻[Root.GetName]上的£pop£§H唯物主义人口§!并且宣扬§Y唯心主义§!。\n约§Y300§!£pop£§H人口§!获得§Y唯物主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Will attempt to dissuade £pop£ §HMaterialist Pops§! on [Root.GetName] and promote §YSpiritualism§!.\nApproximately §Y300§! £pop£ §HPops§! gain the §YMaterialist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:460`

## colony.2011.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2011.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

成功对部分试图反对我们主要思潮的人口进行了重新教导。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

Our attempts at re-directing the parts of the population prone to oppose our core ideals have finally paid off.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:461`

## colony.2011.desc.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2011.desc.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

多亏了我们的精神领袖的不懈努力，我们终于力挽狂澜，吸引来了越来越多的无信仰人士。这些人不限于唯物主义者，甚至乎包括了其他宗教人士。愿他们从此不再误入歧途。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Thanks to the tireless efforts of our spiritual leaders we have finally turned the tide, and are bringing more and more faithless into the fold. Not only materialists, but people of all persuasions have seen the light and are casting away their former beliefs. Pray they will never stray from the righteous path again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:462`

## colony.2011.desc.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2011.desc.material&lang=en)

### 中文

$colony.2011.desc.base$ 我们的宣传机器和计划中一样的运行。不仅仅是唯心主义者，那些骑墙派也明白了理性的可贵，并且为了支持唯物主义抛弃了他们从前的妄想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

$colony.2011.desc.base$ Our propaganda machine has worked as promised. Not only spiritualists, but people of all persuasions are seeing reason and casting aside their former delusions in favor of materialism.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:463`

## colony.2011.a.spirit.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2011.a.spirit.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

全体£pop£§H人口§!失去§Y唯物主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

All £pop£ §HPops§! lose the §YMaterialist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:464`

## colony.2011.a.material.tooltip

### 中文

全体£pop£§H人口§!失去§Y唯心主义§!思潮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

All £pop£ §HPops§! lose the §YSpiritualist§! Ethic.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:465`

## colony.2012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们做出改变[Root.GetName]上人口思想的尝试简直就是搬起石头砸自己的脚。通过团结起来反抗我们的文化镇压，他们获得了更多的追随者，这使他们变得比以前还要强大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

Our attempts to change the minds of our people on [Root.GetName] have backfired horribly. By banding together over our subjugation attempts they have gained even more followers, making them stronger than ever.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:466`

## colony.2012.a.spirit.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2012.a.spirit.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的所有唯物主义的人口获得了§R$opinion_disrespected$§!的修正，效果为：\n£happiness£幸福度§R-40%§!\n\n添加§Y$materialist_attraction$§!的修正，效果为：\n唯物主义思潮吸引力§G+100%§!，时长20年

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

All Materialist Pops on [Root.GetName] gain the §R$opinion_disrespected$§! modifier with the following effects:\n£happiness£ Happiness §R-40%§!\n\n§Y$materialist_attraction$§! modifier added with the following effects:\nMaterialist Ethics Attraction §G+100%§! for 20 years.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:467`

## colony.2012.a.material.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2012.a.material.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的所有唯心主义的人口获得了§R$opinion_disrespected$§!的修正，效果为：\n£happiness£幸福度§R-40%§!\n\n增加§Y$spiritualist_attraction$§!的修正，效果为：\n唯心主义思潮吸引力§G+100%§!，时长20年

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

All Spiritualist Pops on [Root.GetName] gain the §R$opinion_disrespected$§! modifier with the following effects:\n£happiness£ Happiness §R-40%§!\n\n§Y$spiritualist_attraction$§! modifier added with the following effects:\nSpiritualist Ethics Attraction §G+100%§! for 20 years.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:468`

## colony.2015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古怪工厂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

An Odd Factor

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:469`

## colony.2015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一队调查小组在[Root.GetName]上发现了隐藏的工厂。根据建筑构架和磨损程度判断，它源自一个古老文明，这个文明十分擅长建造经得起时间考验的建筑。\n\n我们可以轻松使用这个工厂，增加殖民地的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

A group of investigators have found a hidden factory on [Root.GetName]. The building and its state of decay tells of an old civilization, one that excelled at constructing things that stood the test of time.\n\nOur population would easily be able to use the facility to add to the colony's production output.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:470`

## colony.2015.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2015.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的地表出现一座§S£building£古怪工厂§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

An §S£building£ Odd Factory§! appears on the surface of [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:471`

## colony.2016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上古怪工厂工作的员工突然消失了！我们可以派遣工人替补，或者以防万一，直接将工厂夷为平地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

The crew we assigned to work the odd factory on [Root.GetName] has disappeared! We could send a new workforce to take their place, or we could raze the facility to the ground, making sure that this will not happen again.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:472`

## colony.2016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这仍对我们有用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

It still suits our needs.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:473`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

§R100§! £pop£ §HPops§! have gone missing on [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:474`

## colony.2016.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2016.b&lang=en)

拆毁它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

Demolish it.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:475`

## colony.2016.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2016.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§S£building£古怪工厂§!已被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

The §S£building£ Odd Factory§! is destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:476`

## colony.2017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

到那个古怪工厂工作后，第二队工人也失踪了！所幸，其中一个工人留下了记录。记录显示那里有不少的陷阱，这些陷阱专门用来抓捕毫无防备的工人。\n\n陷阱已经移除，现在应该可以正常使用工厂了。但不论如何解释，民众仍对潜在的致命陷阱忧心忡忡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

A second group of individuals has gone missing after working at the odd factory! Fortunately one of the workers was able to leave a recording behind, revealing that there are traps capturing unsuspecting workers.\n\nThe traps have been disabled and we should be able to use the factory without further issues. However, the populace are none too happy about the potential death trap, no matter what we say.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:477`

### English

The people will get used to it.

## colony.2018.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2018.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于工人触发了某种自毁程序，这个工厂爆炸了。所幸建筑倒塌时没有人员伤亡。\n\n我们应该可以从残骸中回收一些资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:478`

### English

The factory has exploded due to workers triggering some sort of self-destruct program. Luckily no one was hurt before the old construct fell to the ground.\n\nWe should be able to recover some of the production from the wreckage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:482`

## colony.2018.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2018.a&lang=en)

## colony.2019.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2019.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]上的古怪工厂并未出现任何异常。殖民地将维持预计的产出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:483`

### English

No reports of any problems have been issued from the odd factory on [Root.GetName]. It seems like the colony will maintain the projected production estimates.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:487`

## colony.2020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的拆除团队传来消息，[Root.GetName]的工厂已被移除。希望这是好事。\n\n我们应该可以从残骸中回收一些资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:484`

### English

The demolition crew has briefed us that the factory on [Root.GetName] has been successfully removed. Maybe for the better.\n\nWe should be able to recover some of the production from the wreckage.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:488`

## colony.2025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

种群隔阂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

What Separates Us

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:491`

## colony.2025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上，我们的种群和混居的其他种群产生了不和。具体原因未知，可能是因为文化区别、自我封闭或者单单的误解。但很明显，若是放任不管，这些小型冲突就会愈演愈烈。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:488`

### English

Having other species living with our people has caused some friction on [Root.GetName]. Be it cultural differences, xenophobia or a common misunderstanding is hard to tell, but what is sure is that these small encounters could turn to the worse if we do not intervene.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:492`

## colony.2025.phobe.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.phobe.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[Root.GetName]上，混居在我们其中的异星人开始惹是生非了。这引起了我们民众的众怒，每天都有越来越多的争斗彰显。如果坐视不理，那些异星人估计会损失惨重。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:489`

### English

The xenos living among us have begun to cause friction on [Root.GetName]. Our own people are furious, resulting in more and more fights breaking out each day. If we idly stand by the xenos might end up losing more than some bodily fluids.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:493`

## colony.2025.phile.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.phile.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与其他种群共生并非易事。在[Root.GetName]星上，由于无法让外星人合理融入我方社会，一些不和悄然形成。如果仍然不采取行动，或许会产生危机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Having our own species living together with other lifeforms isn't always easy. Some friction has begun to take form on [Root.GetName], surely due to our inability to incorporate the aliens into our society properly. If nothing is done they might be at risk.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:494`

## colony.2025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

分散狂躁大众。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

Distract the angry mob.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:495`

## colony.2025.a.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2025.a.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

隔离外星人与狂躁大众。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

Isolate the xenos from the mob.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:496`

## colony.2025.a.tooltip

## colony.2030.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2030.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现在§Y[Root.GetName]§!的总督已经死亡！有人目击了试图逃跑的犯人，刺客来自§Y[AssassinEmpire.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Our governor on §Y[Root.GetName]§! has been found dead! The perpetrator was caught trying to make an escape, an assassin sent by the §Y[AssassinEmpire.GetName]§!.

## colony.2035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

传染性

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

Infectious

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:514`

## colony.2035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的部分人口聚集区报告表示，民众遭受了史无前例的大规模传染病。这种疾病有多株病原体。被感染的患者几乎无法行动并产生严重呼吸困难。\n\n若疫情不加以控制，[Root.GetName]将迎来末日。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Several population districts on [Root.GetName] are reporting people falling ill, on an unprecedented scale. Anyone affected by one of this disease's many strains can barely move and are succumbed to vile extrusions.\n\nIf left uncontrolled this sickness might be the end of [Root.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:515`

## colony.2035.desc.material

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2035.desc.material&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的一些人口聚集区提交报告表示，该地民众受到了某种严重疾病感染。该疾病传播极为迅速，并有许多不断迅速演化的病原体，如不及时介入将阻碍地区生产及发展。\n\n如不迅速采取措施，殖民地建设将停滞甚至倒退数年。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

Several population districts on [Root.GetName] have filed reports of civilians falling ill to a vicious sickness. It spreads quickly, it has multiple, fast evolving strains, and could halt production and expansion if not taken care of.\n\nIf not acted upon fast the colony might fall, setting us back years in development.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:516`

## colony.2035.desc.spirit

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2035.desc.spirit&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的一些人口聚集区陷入了某种未知且极为可怕的疫情。科学家对此一无所知。由于并发症多样，该疾病一时让人束手无策。而这些并发症最终都会随病毒扩散全身而导致患者神志不清。\n\n如不采取措施，许多无辜灵魂将堕入黑暗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Several population districts on [Root.GetName] have fallen ill to an ungodly new disease, unknown to our scientists. The vile sickness evolves, making it hard to understand, but all types of it makes the mind falter as it festers in the body.\n\nIf no measures are taken we might lose many a soul to the darkness.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:517`

## colony.2035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

建造医院以安置患者并加以研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Build a hospital we can send the ill to for study.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:518`

## colony.2036.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2036.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即便我们竭力试图控制传播风险，[Root.GetName]的疾病看上去比我们预估的更加难以处理。更多人产生了相同病症。我们必须重新思考解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Even though we tried to limit the risk of spreading, the disease on [Root.GetName] seems more difficult to get rid of than we first thought. More individuals have emerged with the same symptoms. We might have to rethink how we approach this issue.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:527`

## colony.2040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

疾病迅速在[Root.GetName]上泛滥成灾。我们需要迅速行动并将感染者送入紧急研究所。希望我们可以找到治疗方案，如果他们能撑到我们找到发病根源的话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

[Root.GetName] is quickly being overrun by the disease. We need to act fast and send a population to the emergency research station. Hopefully we can find a cure, if they survive until we find the root cause.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:532`

## colony.2046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

肆虐[Root.GetName]的疾病已经带走了第一名受害者。我们需要确保急救站中有试验小组对疾病进行研究，从而增加找到治疗方案的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

The disease ravaging [Root.GetName] has claimed its first victim. We need to make sure the emergency clinic has a test group to study, in order to increase the likelihood of finding a cure.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:533`

## colony.2046.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2046.a&lang=en)

### 中文

如果有更多人死亡，及时告知我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

Give me updates if any more die.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:534`

## colony.2046.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2046.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不要告知我最新情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

Don't update me.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:535`

## colony.2047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的又一批感染者逝世了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

Another group of infected falls dead on [Root.GetName] from the disease.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:536`

## colony.2041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的一座急救站在临床试验中成功治愈了一批病患。我们应该派更多病人过去接受治疗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

The emergency clinic on [Root.GetName] seems to have been able to cure its test group from their illness. We should send more there for treatment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:537`

## colony.2041.b.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2041.b.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的一家诊所对患者进行了随机采样分组，并成功治愈了其中一组患者的疾病。我们应当派人们前去诊所以加速确认治疗方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

The clinic on [Root.GetName] has collected random samples from the populace and been able to cure one group's strand of the disease. We should send people to the clinic for faster treatment.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:538`

## colony.2042.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2042.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在成功治疗[Root.GetName]的多名患者之后，我们的研究人员成功合成出疫苗。有了疫苗以后我们就能够迅速治愈全部患者。疫情带来的恐慌终于迎来终结！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

After managing to cure various strands of the illness on [Root.GetName] our research staff has been able to synthesize a vaccine. With this we can quickly cure the entire population and the horror is finally over!

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:539`

## colony.2043.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2043.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的最后一名感染者成功被治愈。看上去疾病在此时已经找不到新的宿主了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

The last of the infected on [Root.GetName] is no more. It appears that the disease cannot spread to any new hosts at this point.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:540`

## colony.2044.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2044.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]成为了一颗死星。[Root.GetPlanetMoon]的人口已经被全部清除。好消息是让人束手无策的疾病也被清除了。我们现在可以重新发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

[Root.GetName] stands empty. The [Root.GetPlanetMoon]'s population has been totally wiped out. The good news is that this must have eradicated even the disease and we should be able to repopulate.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:541`

## colony.2043.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2043.a&lang=en)

### 中文

灾祸终结了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

The scourge is over.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:542`

## colony.2050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

官员失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

Lost to Bureaucracy

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:543`

## colony.2050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acolony.2050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于重大的记录错误，[Root.GetName]部分区域的基础设施维护与住宅服务已趋于落后。人们正居住在较为恶劣的环境之中，似乎已忍不了太久了。要修复这些问题则会耗费我们的时间与精力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/events_3_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

Due to a major clerical error parts of [Root.GetName] has fallen behind in infrastructure maintenance and accommodation services. The people live in rather bleak conditions and will not tolerate it for long. Remedying these problems will cost us time and effort.

Source: `localisation/english/events_3_l_english.yml:544`
